# Board adaptation log: rk3588-evb7-v11

- SoC / family: RK3588 / rockchip-rk3588
- Kernel branch: vendor (6.1, armbian/linux-rockchip rk-6.1-rkr5.1, 6.1.115)
- Status: AMP bare-metal validation PASSED (cpu3 runs firmware; Linux on 7 cores)

## Build & flash (known-good)
- Build: `./compile.sh build BOARD=rk3588-evb7-v11 BRANCH=vendor RELEASE=bookworm BUILD_MINIMAL=yes BUILD_DESKTOP=no`
- Host prep (one-time): install qemu-user-static + binfmt so arm64 rootfs can be
  built on amd64 host (`sudo apt install qemu-user-static binfmt-support` or
  `docker run --privileged --rm tonistiigi/binfmt --install all`)
- Flash (eMMC via MASKROM):
  - `rkdeveloptool db <rockdev>/MiniLoaderAll.bin`
  - `rkdeveloptool wl 0 output/images/Armbian-...Rk3588-evb7-v11_bookworm_vendor_6.1.115_minimal.img`
  - `rkdeveloptool rd`
- Serial: UART2 @ **1500000** 8N1, console=ttyFIQ0
- Boot media: eMMC (SD boot gave no output; direct eMMC flash worked)
- Partial reflash of `boot` / `amp` only (a dts or NuttX change, no full image
  rebuild), the boot.img repack flow, and every cross-compile command for the
  AMP demo: see **A-18**, sections "boot.img 官方打包流程" / "分区表与刷写" /
  "编译命令（全套）". Run `rkdeveloptool ppt` first - partition names are not
  guessable, and `wlx boot` was undocumented until that round.
- After a **full** image flash (`wl 0`) the rootfs is new and nothing AMP-side
  starts by itself. Push the two rpmsg `.ko` to `/userdata` (build artefacts,
  not in git), then run `rk3588-amp-demo/deploy/install.sh` on the board: it
  enables WiFi/BT, enables `amp-rpmsg.service`, and sets the default target to
  multi-user so no desktop holds DRM master. See **A-19**.

### AMP memory region (IMPORTANT - revised after first boot)
- First try loaded firmware at 0x00800000 (EVB1 amp_reserved). RESULT: u-boot
  "Sysmem Error: KERNEL (0x003ff800-0x02a10a00) overlap with baremetal-cpu3
  (0x00800000)" -> u-boot loads kernel at 0x400000 (~44MB) which overlaps 0x800000.
- cpu3 WAS brought up OK ("AMP: Brought up cpu[300] ... entry 0x00800000 ... OK"),
  so the AMP mechanism works; only the load address collided.
- mcu@7a00000 is the Cortex-M0 region; amp-shmem@8200000 overlaps OP-TEE (loads at
  0x08400000). Both unsafe.
- FIX: dedicated reserved region `amp-core@30000000` (0x30000000, 16MB, no-map),
  above kernel/M0/OP-TEE/CMA. amp.its load + amp.ld + dts all set to 0x30000000.

### AMP key facts
- AMP core: cpu_l3, MPIDR 0x300; firmware load addr 0x00800000 (amp_reserved).
- UART5 base 0xfeb80000 (AMP core console).
- u-boot must be OFFICIAL vendor build (rk3588_defconfig + rk-amp.config); mainline
  generic-rk3588 (current board default) has NO CONFIG_AMP -> AMP path needs vendor u-boot.
- AMP requires trust (BL31/trust.img).
- amp.img generation: `<u-boot>/tools/mkimage -f amp.its -E -p 0xe00 amp.img`, flashed to `amp` partition.

## Notes
- DDR/ATF come from Armbian's own rkbin (DDR v1.20_20250926, BL31 v1.48). Official
  BSP rkbin has DDR v1.21 / BL31 v1.54.
- generic-rk3588 u-boot has no PCIe/USB/NVMe/net boot — SD/eMMC boot only.
- AMP validation is done via the OFFICIAL SDK toolchain, not Armbian; restore the
  official firmware baseline before adding the amp partition.

## AMP validation result (official-SDK path) — MILESTONE: PASSED
- u-boot: `AMP: Brought up cpu[300] ... entry 0x30000000 ...OK` (cpu3 started).
- No memory overlap after moving firmware to dedicated amp-core@30000000 region.
- Kernel boots; Linux reaches multi-user; `root@debian:/#` login.
- Linux AMP side up: `rockchip-rpmsg ... platform probe`, `rpmsg host is online`
  (x2), `rockchip-amp` claimed UART5 pins (gpio4-29).
- eMMC repartitioned to p1..p9 (amp partition present).
- Flashing recipe that worked (MASKROM): db loader; ul loader; gpt parameter-amp.txt;
  wlx uboot(AMP)/boot(-amp)/amp/rootfs/oem/userdata; rd.
  (KEY: `ul` needed to install the vendor SPL loader; `db`-only left the old
   Armbian SPL and caused a reset loop.)

## Verifying the AMP core WITHOUT UART5 (shared-memory heartbeat)
EVB7 has no accessible UART5 pins, so the demo also writes a heartbeat into its
own no-map reserved region; Linux reads it via /dev/mem.
- amp1.c writes: 0x30000800 = magic 0x414D5033 ("AMP3"); 0x30000804 = counter++ forever.
- Why safe: 0x30000000 region is `no-map` reserved -> excluded from Linux System RAM,
  Linux never uses/touches it, so the AMP core owns it exclusively (writes don't
  affect Linux). Because it's not "System RAM", STRICT_DEVMEM still allows /dev/mem
  reads of it. Both sides hit DRAM directly (AMP MMU/cache off; /dev/mem uncached) -> coherent.
- Read from Linux (root):
    python3 -c 'import mmap,os,struct,time; f=os.open("/dev/mem",os.O_RDONLY|os.O_SYNC); \
    m=mmap.mmap(f,0x1000,mmap.MAP_SHARED,mmap.PROT_READ,offset=0x30000000); \
    rd=lambda o:struct.unpack("<I",m[o:o+4])[0]; print(hex(rd(0x800))); \
    a=rd(0x804); time.sleep(1); print(a, rd(0x804))'
  or: `busybox devmem 0x30000800 32` (magic) / `busybox devmem 0x30000804 32` (read twice).
- PASS = magic==0x414d5033 AND counter increments -> cpu3 is executing our firmware.
- amp1.bin is now 336 bytes (heartbeat added); amp.img rebuilt (load 0x30000000);
  reflash only the `amp` partition: `rkdeveloptool db <spl_loader>; wlx amp amp.img; rd`.

### FINAL RESULT: heartbeat counter increments (0x15 -> 0x2E observed) after stack fix
-> cpu3 continuously executing the AMP firmware. AMP bare-metal path fully validated.

### Heartbeat debug: counter stuck at 1 -> missing stack pointer (FIXED)
- First heartbeat run: magic 0x30000800 = 0x414D5033 (correct, cpu3 executed our
  code), but 0x30000804 counter stuck at 0x00000001.
- Cause: bare-metal firmware never set SP. objdump showed `sub sp,sp,#0x10` in the
  worker; cpu3 starts with an undefined SP, so magic/count=1 (absolute stores)
  succeeded but the delay loop's stack access faulted -> cpu3 hung after 1 iter.
- FIX: added amp_start.S entry that sets `sp = 0x30200000` (inside amp-core region)
  before `bl amp_main`. amp1.c worker renamed _start -> amp_main. build.sh links
  amp_start.o + amp1.o. amp1.bin now 368B; _start verified: mov sp,#0x30200000; bl amp_main.
- NOTE for .S via heredoc: tabs/spaces get stripped -> use printf per-line (mnemonic
  and operands must keep a space, e.g. `movz x0, #0x3020, lsl #16`).

## Desktop / GPU (Mali G610) — FIXED
- Official rootfs already has GNOME + weston + Xorg + libmali (libmali-valhall-g610-g29p1)
  -> no need to install a desktop; just start it.
- Our AMP kernel (rockchip_linux_defconfig + amp) had the WRONG Mali driver:
  MALI400 + MALI_MIDGARD =y, but `# CONFIG_MALI_BIFROST is not set`. RK3588 G610 is
  Valhall/Bifrost -> weston failed: "No mali devices found" / EGL_NOT_INITIALIZED.
- FIX: kernel fragment arch/arm64/configs/rk3588_g610.config:
    # CONFIG_MALI400 is not set
    # CONFIG_MALI_MIDGARD is not set
    CONFIG_MALI_BIFROST=y
    CONFIG_MALI_CSF_SUPPORT=y
    CONFIG_MALI_BIFROST_DEVFREQ=y
    CONFIG_MALI_PLATFORM_NAME="rk"
  merge_config + olddefconfig + rebuild rk3588-evb7-v11-linux-amp.img; reflash boot.
- CONFIRMED on target: `mali fb000000.gpu: GPU identified as ... arch 10.8.6 r0p0`
  (G610), no more "No mali devices found", `[OK] Started S49weston.service`,
  DSI panel 1080x1920p60 bound -> desktop up with GPU accel.
- To autostart desktop: `systemctl enable S49weston` (weston) or
  `systemctl set-default graphical.target` + gdm (GNOME).

## WiFi/BT (AP6398S) — FIXED
- WiFi chip AP6398S (BCM4359, SDIO bcmdhd); BT on /dev/ttyS9 (uart9), FW BCM4359C0.hcd.
- Symptom after custom kernel: no wlan0. rockchip_linux_defconfig builds bcmdhd
  BUILT-IN -> it inits at ~3.2s BEFORE rfkill-wlan probes -> WL_REG_ON=-1 ->
  sdio_register_driver timeout -> "Failed to load driver".
- Root cause of the config confusion: `menuconfig BCMDHD` is a BOOL gate (default y);
  the real module/built-in switch is `config AP6XXX` (tristate) via
  `obj-$(CONFIG_AP6XXX) += bcmdhd/` in rkwifi/Makefile.
- FIX (kernel fragment rk3588_wifibt.config): CONFIG_AP6XXX=m (build bcmdhd as a
  loadable module = official design), WL_ROCKCHIP=y, BCMDHD=y (gate), BCMDHD_SDIO=y,
  WIFI_LOAD_DRIVER_WHEN_KERNEL_BOOTUP off, WIFI_BUILD_MODULE off.
- Deploy: reflash boot.img; place our bcmdhd.ko in /lib/modules/ (flat path; official
  rootfs has no version dir, no depmod -> load via insmod full path).
- Auto-load at boot: systemd oneshot `bcmdhd.service` (ExecStart=/sbin/insmod
  /lib/modules/bcmdhd.ko, After=systemd-modules-load.service). rfkill-wlan is built-in
  and ready by then, so no race.
- CONFIRMED: `wl_android_wifi_on: Success`, wlan0 up + scanning; auto-appears on reboot.

### WiFi can scan but NetworkManager can't connect — FIXED (disable NM MAC randomization)
- Symptom: `nmcli dev wifi connect` fails with `config-failed` /
  `The device could not be readied for configuration`; sometimes
  `No network with SSID ... found` even though the AP is visible.
- Isolation: manual `wpa_supplicant -B -i wlan0 -c wpa.conf -D nl80211` +
  `udhcpc` connects and gets an IP immediately (5G, ping OK) -> hardware,
  firmware, nvram (fallback /nvram_ap6398s.txt OK), country code CN/38, and
  the password are all fine. Problem is 100% NetworkManager.
- Root cause: NM WiFi MAC randomization. NM tries to set a random MAC on
  wlan0 before scan/connect (`set-hw-addr: new MAC ... (scanning)` /
  `(preserve)`), but bcmdhd rejects changing the MAC while the iface is up
  -> `set-hw-addr: not successfully set` -> `state change: prepare -> failed
  (reason 'config-failed')`. Every scan/activation dies. (This is also why
  earlier logs showed the MAC constantly changing.)
- Note: firmware auxiliary loads that look scary but are HARMLESS:
  `nvram_ap6398sa.txt err -2` is only the FIRST attempt; dhd falls back to
  `/nvram_ap6398s.txt` ("NVRAM succeeded"). `clm_bcm4359c0_ag.blob err -2` ->
  `dhd_apply_default_clm: Ignore clm file` uses the firmware built-in CLM
  (9.7.8). Neither breaks WiFi.
- FIX (rootfs config, no kernel/reflash): /etc/NetworkManager/conf.d/wifi-no-randmac.conf
    [device]
    wifi.scan-rand-mac-address=no
    [connection]
    wifi.cloned-mac-address=permanent
  Then `systemctl restart NetworkManager`, delete any stale wifi profiles,
  `nmcli dev wifi connect "<SSID>" password "<pw>"`.
- CONFIRMED: `Device 'wlan0' successfully activated`, wlan0 connected, got
  DHCP IP 192.168.31.157, ping 8.8.8.8 OK.
- Related nmcli gotcha: a stale connection profile missing key-mgmt gives
  `802-11-wireless-security.key-mgmt: property is missing` on reconnect;
  fix = delete the profile (or `nmcli con modify <name>
  802-11-wireless-security.key-mgmt wpa-psk`) then reconnect.
- BT: kernel side ready (BT=y, HCIUART=y/H4, BT_BCM=y/btbcm built-in) + BCM4359C0.hcd
  in rootfs; chip AP6398S (BCM4359) on uart9 = /dev/ttyS9 (dts `wireless-bluetooth`
  rfkill node, bt_port="/dev/ttyS9"). BT now WORKING.

### BT root cause + fix — FIXED (userspace patchram attach)
- Symptom: BT_RFKILL powers BT on and retries (log t=47/49) but no hci0 ever appears.
- Root cause: the official `/usr/bin/wifibt-init.sh` `start_bt()` does
  `insmod_bt; return 0 # BT initialized by kernel` — it SKIPS the userspace attach,
  assuming a serdev (kernel 6.12) BT. But our kernel has `# CONFIG_SERIAL_DEV_BUS is
  not set` (no serdev) and the dts uses the rockchip rfkill `wireless-bluetooth`
  node (NOT a uart serdev subnode). So nothing ever downloaded the .hcd / attached
  H4 -> no hci0.
- Kernel already has the classic userspace-attach deps built-in: BT_HCIUART=y,
  BT_HCIUART_H4=y, BT_BCM=y (btbcm). So `brcm_patchram_plus1 --enable_hci` is all
  that's needed (download BCM4359C0.hcd, set H4 ldisc -> hci0).
- FIX: dedicated systemd unit `brcm-bt.service` (Type=simple, foreground so it
  holds ttyS9 and keeps hci0 attached):
    ExecStartPre=-/usr/sbin/rfkill unblock bluetooth
    ExecStart=/usr/bin/brcm_patchram_plus1 --enable_hci --no2bytes \
      --use_baudrate_for_download --tosleep 200000 --baudrate 1500000 \
      --patchram /lib/firmware/brcm/ /dev/ttyS9
    ExecStartPost=poll `hciconfig hci0 up` up to 10s
    After=wifibt-init.service systemd-rfkill.service
- Deploy: `cp brcm-bt.service /etc/systemd/system/; systemctl daemon-reload;
  systemctl enable --now brcm-bt.service`.
- CONFIRMED on target: hci0 up, BT working.
- File: rk3588-amp-demo/brcm-bt.service (external SDK tree, committed there).

## FINAL: rk3588-evb7-v11 runs AMP + desktop + WiFi on the OFFICIAL BSP stack
Decision: do NOT port to Armbian (vendor kernel forces vendor userspace libmali/bcmdhd,
negating Armbian rootfs benefit). Ship on official kernel 6.1.141 + vendor AMP u-boot +
official Debian rootfs, with our kernel fragments (amp dts, g610, wifibt) + amp.img/partition.

## Known minor issues
- userdata (p9) must be (re)flashed with official userdata.img after repartition,
  else systemd drops to emergency mode (`local-fs.target` fails).
- Pin conflict: UART5 (uart5m0, gpio4-29) is claimed by rockchip-amp, so the SD
  card slot (fe2c0000.mmc/sdmmc) cannot use it. Boot from eMMC unaffected.

## NuttX (openvela) AMP port on cpu_l3 — MILESTONE 1 REACHED (boots & prints)
Goal: run NuttX as the AMP remote-core firmware on cpu_l3, replacing the
bare-metal amp1.bin. Source tree: /media/1t/openvela/work/nuttx (openvela).

### What was created (external openvela git tree: work/nuttx)
- Chip layer arch/arm64/src/rk3588/ + arch/arm64/include/rk3588/ (copied from
  rk3399, retargeted):
  - GICD=0xfe600000, GICR=0xfe680000 (RK3588 GICv3), GICR stride 0x20000
  - RAMBANK1=0x30000000 / 16MB; CONFIG_LOAD_BASE=0x30000000
  - UART2=0xfeb50000, IRQ 365 (GIC_SPI 333 + 32); lowputc/serial retargeted
  - get_cpu_id keeps Aff0 (ubfx #0,#8): RK3588 每核独立 cluster (Aff1=core,
    Aff0=0) so cpu_l3 (MPIDR 0x300) => id 0 => head.S treats it as PRIMARY in
    UP mode (no fail). This dissolved the "primary判定" concern.
  - rk3588_boot.c g_mmu_regions: DEVICE(0xf8000000/128MB) + DRAM(0x30000000/16MB)
    + AMP_SHMEM(0x31000000/4MB) so the core can touch the shared-mem heartbeat.
  - NR_IRQS raised 181 -> 512 (RK3588 GICv3 has far more INTIDs; GIC init walks
    all of them via up_prioritize_irq -> assert at arm64_gicv3.c:664 otherwise).
  - rk3588_serial.c: a64_uart_divisor/a64_uart_wait marked __attribute__((unused))
    because SUPPRESS_UART_CONFIG drops their only caller (-Werror).
  - Registered in arch/arm64/Kconfig (chip def + name + source) and boards/Kconfig.
- Board layer boards/arm64/rk3588/evb7-amp/ (copied from pinephonepro):
  - scripts/dramboot.ld links at 0x30000000
  - configs/nsh/defconfig: RK3588 chip/board, RAM 0x30000000/16MB, UP (SMP off),
    SUPPRESS_UART_CONFIG=y (borrow Linux's UART2 1500000 8N1, don't reconfigure),
    NSH + hpwork.
  - evb7_amp_bringup.c: prints "[AMP] hello from NuttX on cpu_l3" and starts a
    kthread heartbeat writing magic 0x414D5033 @0x31000000 + counter @0x31000004
    (moved OFF 0x30000800 which is now inside the NuttX image).
- Build: openvela toolchain work/prebuilts/gcc/linux-x86_64/aarch64-none-elf
  (gcc 13.4). `tools/configure.sh -l evb7-amp:nsh` then `make`. nuttx.bin links
  at 0x30000000 (verified via readelf LOAD vaddr).
- Package: rk3588-amp-demo/amp-nuttx.its (data=nuttx.bin, cpu=0x300, load=
  0x30000000) -> mkimage -> amp.img -> flash `amp` partition (same path as the
  bare-metal demo).

### Verified on target (serial log)
- `AMP: Brought up cpu[300] ...OK` -> OP-TEE `Secondary CPU 3 switching to
  normal world boot` -> `Ready to Boot Primary CPU` / `Boot from EL1` /
  `Boot to C runtime` -> `[AMP] hello from NuttX on cpu_l3` +
  `[AMP] NuttX heartbeat running @0x31000000`. Linux boots normally afterwards.
- sysmem shows `nuttx-cpu3` loaded at 0x30000000-0x30049000 (~292KB).
- => chip/board/link/MMU/primary-cpu/EL1/C-runtime/GIC-basic-init/first-schedule
  ALL working. NuttX prints via the shared UART2 too.

### Known issue -> MILESTONE 2 (interrupts/timer)
- Heartbeat counter (0x31000004) is STUCK AT 1: the thread ran once, printed,
  did count++ =1, then `usleep()` never returns because the ARM arch timer tick
  never fires. NuttX arm64 uses the VIRTUAL timer (CONFIG_ARM_TIMER_VIRTUAL_IRQ,
  PPI 27). On the AMP core (entered EL1 via BL31 secondary-cpu path) the timer
  IRQ is not being delivered — likely GICR(redistributor) PPI-enable / per-cpu
  rdist location for the 8-core RK3588 layout, or virtual-timer needs EL2
  CNTVOFF setup that the AMP entry path didn't do.
- Next: (a) confirm scheduler-vs-timer by temporarily using a busy-loop delay
  heartbeat; (b) check GICv3 rdist selection for MPIDR 0x300; (c) consider EL1
  physical timer (CNTP, PPI 30) instead of virtual for the AMP core.

### MILESTONE 2 (arch timer) — root-caused & fixed the redistributor selection
- Symptom recap: heartbeat stuck at 1 -> arch timer tick never fired.
- ROOT CAUSE: arm64_gicv3.c `arm64_gic_init()` set the per-cpu redistributor as
  `g_gic_rdists = CONFIG_GICR_BASE + up_cpu_index() * CONFIG_GICR_OFFSET`.
  On AMP, NuttX runs on cpu_l3 but up_cpu_index()==0, so it programmed cpu0's
  redistributor (0xfe680000) instead of cpu_l3's (0xfe6c0000). cpu_l3's own
  timer PPI was never enabled -> no tick.
- FIX (arm64_gicv3.c, common but GICv3-standard & SMP-safe): locate this core's
  redistributor by matching GICR_TYPER[63:32] Affinity to our MPIDR, walking
  frames until match or GICR_TYPER.Last. cpu_l3 (MPIDR 0x300) -> 0xfe6c0000.
- RESULT: heartbeat counter now INCREMENTS (observed 1 -> 12). Timer + scheduler
  genuinely run on cpu_l3. NR_IRQS=512 confirmed correct (Linux reports "GICv3:
  480 SPIs implemented" => 32+480=512).
- REMAINING (milestone 2b / GIC coexistence): counter then STOPS at 12 (~12 s),
  which coincides with the Linux kernel's GICv3 probe taking over. Linux resets/
  reconfigures the (global) GICD ("Using split EOI/Deactivate mode") and this
  cuts cpu_l3's timer interrupt delivery (NuttX doesn't crash — no assert/dump —
  it just sleeps forever after the last usleep). No NuttX crash output.
  Both u-boot, NuttX and Linux share UART2 0xfeb50000 (Linux earlycon too).
- Next diagnostics: add a second, timer-independent busy-loop counter
  (@0x31000008) alongside the usleep counter (@0x31000004): if the busy counter
  keeps rising past the point the timer counter freezes, cpu_l3 is alive and only
  the timer IRQ was cut by Linux (points to GIC coexistence work); if both freeze,
  the core was hung.

### MILESTONE 2b diagnosis result: BOTH counters freeze -> cpu_l3 hard-hangs
- Added busy-loop counter @0x31000008 (no timer). On target BOTH froze:
  timer counter @0x31000004 ~12, busy counter @0x31000008 =129. No NuttX crash
  output (no assert/abort/dump).
- Conclusion: NOT just a lost timer tick — cpu_l3 STOPS executing instructions
  entirely, right when Linux's GICv3 driver probes and resets the global GICD.
  Most likely cpu_l3 is stuck in an interrupt storm / ISR lockup (spurious or
  timer PPI it can no longer EOI after Linux mangled GIC state), so even the
  busy thread never runs again.
- This is the fundamental AMP<->Linux GIC coexistence problem. Linux's GICv3
  driver assumes it owns the GIC and resets GICD on probe. Rockchip's official
  AMP uses u-boot setup_sync_bits_for_linux + a Linux-side rockchip-amp driver
  to coordinate GIC init so the two don't clobber each other; our NuttX has no
  such coordination.
- Direction options (pick one):
  (1) Diagnose: add a bounded print of the INTID in arm64 IRQ dispatch +
      sync-exception ESR/FAR, reflash, see exactly what hits cpu_l3 at hang.
  (2) Harden NuttX IRQ path: safely EOI/ignore unexpected INTIDs so a Linux GIC
      reset can't lock the core.
  (3) Coordinate like Rockchip AMP: keep Linux from resetting the parts of GIC
      cpu_l3 relies on (study rockchip-amp driver + the sync-bits handshake).
  (4) Re-init GIC on cpu_l3 after Linux is up (detect and recover).
- Milestone 1 (boot/print) and the timer fix itself remain solid; this is the
  next hard problem.

### MILESTONE 2b cont: physical timer also freezes; found Linux-side AMP GIC setup
- Switched NuttX arch timer from virtual (CNTV, PPI27/INTID27) to EL1 PHYSICAL
  (CNTP, PPI30/INTID30) under CONFIG_ARCH_CHIP_RK3588 (arm64_arch_timer.c) to
  avoid CNTVOFF dependence. On target INTID confirmed 0x1E=30 (physical), but
  cpu_l3 STILL freezes at ~12s (timer counter 12, busy 122, IRQ-entered 37747,
  all frozen). => problem is NOT the timer type; it's GIC-level at Linux takeover.
- Counters are FROZEN (not climbing) => cpu_l3 STOPS executing, not a live storm.
- Linux side inspection (official kernel 6.1.141):
  - CONFIG_ROCKCHIP_AMP=y IS set; irq-gic-v3.c has rockchip_amp_check_amp_irq()
    guards (skip AMP irqs in mask/unmask/eoi/set_type) and preserves AMP irq
    routing in gic_dist_init (rockchip_amp_need_init_amp_irq / get_irq_aff).
  - rk3588-amp.dtsi already has the rockchip,amp node with amp-irqs routing
    GPIO(314-318), UART5(368), MAILBOX(100) SPIs to cpu_l3 (affinity 3,0) via
    GIC_AMP_IRQ_CFG_ROUTE(). These are for the ORIGINAL M0/bare-metal demo,
    NOT what NuttX uses, and do NOT include the timer.
  - Driver drivers/soc/rockchip/rockchip_amp.c parses amp-cpus (id/entry/mode/
    boot-on) and amp-irqs.
- LEADING HYPOTHESIS: amp-irqs routes GPIO/UART5/MAILBOX SPIs to cpu_l3 and may
  enable them. NuttX has NO handlers for these. Once Linux is up, these devices
  (esp. GPIO) raise interrupts that get delivered to cpu_l3 -> NuttX hits an
  unregistered SPI -> lockup. (Timer being the LAST recorded INTID is because
  it's the highest-rate/priority; the fatal traffic may be the orphan SPIs.)
- Next: verify by removing/emptying amp-irqs (or scoping it to only what NuttX
  handles) in the -amp.dts, rebuild boot.img, and see if cpu_l3 survives Linux.
  Bare-metal demo survived precisely because it uses NO interrupts.

### MILESTONE 2b: SPI-storm hypothesis RULED OUT; hang mechanism still open
- rockchip_amp.c does NOT enable/route GIC irqs itself (no writel/GICD/enable_irq);
  it only parses amp-irqs. irq-gic-v3.c gic_dist_init only sets IROUTER for AMP
  irqs (routing), does NOT enable them. So the amp-irqs SPIs (GPIO/UART5/MAILBOX)
  are routed-but-not-enabled -> they never fire -> they do NOT bombard cpu_l3.
  Consistent with "last INTID = timer" (NuttX only ever services the timer).
- So cpu_l3's freeze at Linux takeover is NOT: timer type (both freeze), SPI
  storm (ruled out), nor a fatal CPU exception (no up_dump_register output — a
  sync exception WOULD print, as seen earlier at the gicv3 assert).
- CONFIG_ROCKCHIP_AMP=y is set; rockchip_amp_check_amp_irq() makes Linux skip
  the amp-irqs list in mask/unmask/eoi — but the timer PPI is NOT in that list.
  Timer is a per-cpu PPI (GICR + CPU interface) that Linux shouldn't touch on
  cpu_l3 anyway (cpu_l3 isn't a Linux possible-cpu).
- Mechanism still unknown: cpu_l3 stops executing (even the busy-loop thread) at
  ~12s with no crash print. Candidates left: WFI-sleep-death (all threads block,
  idle WFIs, timer never wakes it), a silent lockup, or a global action by Linux.
- Recommended next (software, 1 reflash): add liveness probes to shared-mem:
  (a) idle-loop counter (is the core WFI-idle but alive?), (b) sync/irq exception
  ESR/ELR recorder (catch a fault even if the UART print path fails). This
  distinguishes WFI-death vs exception vs dead-loop before resorting to JTAG.
- NOTE: Milestone 1 (boot/print/load) and the timer fix (runs 12 s) stand; this
  Linux-coexistence hang is the remaining hard problem.


## MILESTONE 2 ACHIEVED: NuttX runs STABLY on cpu_l3, coexists with Linux
- Switched the heartbeat/busy threads to print each tick DIRECTLY to UART2 via
  polled MMIO (non-cacheable device memory), instead of only writing shared RAM.
- Serial log now shows `[AMP] tick N` (1 Hz, timer) and `[AMP] busy N` (~5 Hz,
  timer-independent) BOTH climbing continuously: reached tick 104 / busy 288 and
  still rising at end of log, right through the Linux GIC probe and full boot.
  No crash output. cpu_l3 runs indefinitely and coexists with Linux.
- => The earlier "freeze at 12/122" was a CACHE-COHERENCY ARTIFACT, NOT a hang:
  NuttX maps amp-shmem (0x31000000) as MT_NORMAL (cacheable); cpu_l3's heartbeat
  writes sat in its cache and were never flushed to DRAM, so Linux reading DRAM
  via /dev/mem saw a stale value. UART MMIO is non-cacheable -> shows the truth.
  cpu_l3 was executing the whole time.
- The GICv3 redistributor fix (locate by MPIDR affinity) was real & necessary
  (otherwise the timer tick / `tick N` would never advance). EL1 physical timer
  is retained (correct for an AMP core; independent of CNTVOFF).
- FOLLOW-UP (for reliable shared-mem + future rpmsg): make amp-shmem coherent
  either by mapping it non-cacheable (MT_NORMAL_NC/MT_DEVICE) on the NuttX side,
  or by explicit cache clean after each write. Heartbeat-via-UART already proves
  liveness; this only matters for shared-memory data paths (rpmsg vrings).
- STATUS: NuttX AMP on cpu_l3 = boots, prints, scheduler + timer run, stable
  alongside Linux. Milestones 1 & 2 DONE. Next optional: shared-mem coherency,
  then NSH over a dedicated UART / rpmsg to Linux.


### Shared-mem cache coherency FIXED (prereq for rpmsg)
- amp-shmem (0x31000000/4MB) MMU mapping changed MT_NORMAL -> MT_NORMAL_NC
  (non-cacheable) in rk3588_boot.c. cpu_l3 writes now hit DRAM immediately.
- CONFIRMED on target: Linux /dev/mem reads now increment in real time,
  matching the UART heartbeat: 0x31000004 (timer) 32->35, 0x31000008 (busy)
  198->202 while polling. The earlier "frozen at 12" is gone.
- This is the prerequisite for rpmsg: its vrings live in this shared window and
  need both cores + Linux to see writes coherently.
- Milestones 1 & 2 + shared-mem coherency all DONE. NuttX AMP on cpu_l3 is a
  stable, Linux-coexisting RTOS with a working cross-core shared-memory path.


### rpmsg groundwork: KEY FACTS discovered from Linux side (for future rpmsg)
- NuttX AMP core stays stable while kicking: serial shows `[AMP] tick N (kick
  mbox)` climbing to 112+, shared-mem coherent. cpu_l3 solid.
- Linux mailbox0 (fec60000.mailbox) DOES register IRQs: GICv3 95 & 96 (Linux
  irq 127/128). `rockchip-mailbox: version 0x0100, tx_dir: A2B, tx_done: poll,
  poll_period 1000`. So Linux TX=A2B (poll for tx-done), RX via IRQ 95/96.
- BUT those mailbox IRQ counts stayed 0 -> our cpu_l3 write to B2A_CMD(0)
  (0xfec60030) did NOT trigger Linux. Likely B2A_INTEN for that channel isn't
  enabled by Linux, or a specific trigger sequence/register is needed. (Min
  mailbox doorbell not yet confirmed.)
- CRITICAL for real rpmsg — the vring/buffer addresses are FIXED by Linux and
  are NOT in amp-shmem:
    rockchip-rpmsg vdev0: vring0 0x7c00000, vring1 0x7c08000
    reserved: rpmsg@7c00000 (4MB), rpmsg-dma@8000000 (2MB)
  So a NuttX rptun must place its vrings at 0x7c00000 (Linux->NuttX) and
  0x7c08000 (NuttX->Linux), buffers around 0x8000000 — matching rk3588-amp.dtsi,
  NOT our 0x31000000 heartbeat window.
- `rpmsg host is online` -> Linux waits for the remote to announce.
- Next for rpmsg: (1) figure out the correct mailbox kick that Linux's RX IRQ
  95/96 sees (study rockchip-mailbox startup / B2A_INTEN enable); (2) build
  NuttX rptun with vrings at 0x7c00000/0x7c08000 + resource table matching
  rockchip rpmsg; (3) OpenAMP. This is a sizable next phase.


### rpmsg MAILBOX DOORBELL WORKS — Linux receives cpu_l3's kick + data
- Fix: cpu_l3 must write BOTH B2A_DAT(0) then B2A_CMD(0) (writing CMD latches
  B2A_STATUS bit0 -> raises Linux's B2A IRQ; DAT carries the payload). Writing
  CMD only (earlier) did nothing.
- CONFIRMED on target — Linux dmesg:
    rockchip-rpmsg 7c00000.rpmsg: rpmsg master rx cb: mailbox data error 0x35!
    ...0x36 0x37 0x38 0x39 0x3a (incrementing = our heartbeat count in B2A_DAT)
  => cpu_l3's mailbox kick reaches Linux's rpmsg RX callback AND the DAT payload
  is read correctly. Doorbell + data path fully proven.
- "mailbox data error" reveals the Linux RX protocol: B2A_DAT must carry a valid
  VRING BUFFER INDEX (Linux rpmsg uses it to fetch the message from the vring),
  not arbitrary data. So the full path is:
    cpu_l3 writes message into vring0 buffer @0x7c00000, puts the buffer index
    in B2A_DAT(0), writes B2A_CMD(0) -> Linux rpmsg pulls the message.
- Both halves of rpmsg now have working foundations: shared memory (coherent) +
  mailbox notification (Linux sees the kick + payload).
- NEXT: build proper vrings at 0x7c00000/0x7c08000 + resource table matching
  rockchip rpmsg, put a real buffer index in B2A_DAT, then wire OpenAMP/rptun.


### rpmsg MAILBOX HANDSHAKE ACCEPTED by Linux (data error gone)
- cpu_l3 now kicks with the correct handshake: B2A_DAT(0)=RPMSG_MBOX_MAGIC
  (0x524D5347 "RMSG"), B2A_CMD(0)=link_id (0x03, rockchip,link-id).
- RESULT on target: the per-second "rpmsg master rx cb: mailbox data error"
  is GONE (0 occurrences, was one per tick). Linux rk_rpmsg_rx_callback now
  passes the data==MAGIC check, reads link_id, marks the remote ready and
  fires vring_interrupt(vq0). NuttX keeps running (270+ [AMP] lines).
- Magic/link-id come from Linux headers/dts:
  include/linux/rpmsg/rockchip_rpmsg.h  RPMSG_MBOX_MAGIC = 0x524D5347
  rk3588-amp.dtsi  rockchip,link-id = <0x03>
- rpmsg foundations now ALL in place: coherent shared mem + working mailbox
  doorbell + accepted handshake. Remaining: real vring data transfer via
  OpenAMP/rptun (vrings at 0x7c00000/0x7c08000, buffers @0x8000000, resource
  table matching rockchip rpmsg). The manual kick already speaks the protocol.


### rptun / OpenAMP integration — BUILT + PACKAGED (pending hardware test)
- Wired the NuttX rptun (OpenAMP virtio remote) driver so the manual mailbox
  kick is replaced by a real rpmsg tunnel. Files (arch/arm64/src/rk3588/):
    - rk3588_rptun.c    rptun_ops: is_master=false, is_autostart=true;
      notify() writes B2A_DAT(magic)+B2A_CMD(link_id) on mailbox0 (the proven
      doorbell); ISR on A2B irq (SPI 100 -> NuttX irq 132) reads/clears
      A2B_STATUS and calls callback(RPTUN_NOTIFY_ALL); rk3588_rptun_init()
      enables A2B_INTEN ch0, irq_attach(132), rptun_initialize().
    - rk3588_rsctable.c resource table (mirrors mx8mp layout): NUM_VRINGS=2,
      RL_BUFFER_COUNT=64, VRING_ALIGN=0x1000, vring0=0x07c00000,
      vring1=0x07c08000 (VRING_SIZE=0x8000), carveout base=0x08000000/2MB,
      resource-table copy dst=0x07c0f000. Matches rk3588-amp.dtsi exactly.
    - rk3588_addrenv.c  identity up_addrenv_pa_to_va / _va_to_pa (VA==PA on our
      flat MMU mapping) — required by rpmsg_virtio + libmetal io.c at link time.
- Make.defs: under CONFIG_RPTUN builds rk3588_rptun.c + rk3588_rsctable.c +
  rk3588_addrenv.c.
- rk3588_boot.c: added MMU region AMP_RPMSG (0x07c00000, 6MB, MT_NORMAL_NC)
  covering vrings + buffer pool (coherent, matching the shmem approach).
- defconfig (evb7-amp:nsh): +CONFIG_RPTUN=y (auto-selects RPMSG_VIRTIO, RPMSG,
  OPENAMP, SCHED_WORKQUEUE) and +CONFIG_RPMSG_CHAR=y (/dev/rpmsg endpoints).
- evb7_amp_bringup.c: calls rk3588_rptun_init("rpmsg","linux") under
  CONFIG_RPTUN; the manual heartbeat mailbox kick is #ifndef CONFIG_RPTUN so it
  no longer fights the OpenAMP negotiation. Liveness threads (busy/tick) kept.
- BUILD: `make -j4` clean (EXIT=0), nuttx.bin 348160 B, amp.img packaged via
  mkimage (load 0x30000000, AArch64). NOT yet flashed/tested on hardware.
- NEXT: flash amp partition, watch UART2 for "[AMP] rptun init ok", check Linux
  for the virtio/rpmsg endpoint (/dev/rpmsg*, dmesg virtio_rpmsg) and test a
  round-trip message. Likely iteration on vring layout / virtio handshake since
  rockchip rpmsg uses fixed dts vring addrs and does not read our rsctable.


### rptun CRASH FIXED — root cause: writing resource table in read-only .rodata
- Symptom: rptun thread paniced immediately (arm64_fatal.c:571, unhandled sync
  exception) right after "[AMP] hello". Register dump was useless (PC always
  showed _assert because assert.c:906 up_saveusercontext overwrites the regs).
- Bisected with temporary raw-UART traces in rk3588_rptun.c + OpenAMP
  (remoteproc.c/rsc_table_parser.c). Trace sequence pinned the fault to
  handle_vdev_rsc (parse of the FIRST resource, idx=0 type=3 RSC_VDEV), NOT a
  stack overflow (bumping RPTUN_STACKSIZE 4K->16K->32K->64K did not help).
- ROOT CAUSE: handle_vdev_rsc WRITES back allocated notify ids:
    vdev_rsc->notifyid = ...;  vring_rsc->notifyid = ...;
  get_resource() returned &g_rk3588_rsc_table, a `const` in .rodata. NuttX
  arm64 arm64_mmu_init maps .rodata READ-ONLY (g_mmu_nxrt_config, MT_RODATA),
  overriding the chip's coarse RW DRAM mapping. The write => permission fault
  => panic. (mx8mp avoids this by returning a writable shmem copy, not the
  const template — the detail I had missed.)
- FIX: get_resource() now returns the WRITABLE copy at RK3588_RSC_TABLE_BASE
  (0x07c0f000, inside the AMP_RPMSG MT_NORMAL_NC RW carveout).
  rk3588_copy_rsc_table() memcpys the template there and returns that pointer;
  rk3588_rptun.c points priv->shmem at it.
- VERIFIED on target: NO more panic. Full happy path prints:
    parse done, carveout mmap ok, callback registered, [AMP] rptun init ok,
    [AMP] hb start. rptun then enters the create_devices retry loop.
- Linux side confirms matching setup:
    rockchip-rpmsg 7c00000.rpmsg: rpdev vdev0: vring0 0x7c00000 vring1 0x7c08000
    virtio_rpmsg_bus virtio0: rpmsg host is online
- REMAINING handshake gap: rptun_create_device (NuttX = DEVICE role) waits for
  VIRTIO_CONFIG_STATUS_DRIVER_OK in the vdev status, but rockchip Linux never
  writes our resource table (uses fixed dts vrings), so it looped forever
  (that was the is_master flood in the log). Applied fix (pending hw test):
  pre-set vdev status = 0x04 (DRIVER_OK) in the resource table so the DEVICE
  side proceeds to create its virtio device and send the NS announcement; Linux
  is "online" from early boot so the driver is effectively ready.
- Also: removed all temporary debug traces from rk3588_rptun.c and the two
  OpenAMP files (restored to upstream). RPTUN_STACKSIZE left at 65536 (arm64
  OpenAMP is stack-hungry; harmless headroom).


### rptun/rpmsg TRANSPORT FULLY UP — no crash, both sides online (VERIFIED)
- Two more resource-table fixes after the .rodata fix, both stemming from the
  fact that rockchip Linux never reads/writes our resource table (it uses fixed
  dts vrings), so anything a normal virtio DRIVER would write we must pin
  ourselves:
  1. status = VIRTIO_CONFIG_STATUS_DRIVER_OK (0x04): else rptun_create_device
     spins on -EAGAIN (was the is_master flood).
  2. gfeatures = dfeatures = F_NS|F_CPUNAME (0x9): the OpenAMP auto-negotiate
     that sets gfeatures=dfeatures runs ONLY for the DRIVER role; for our
     DEVICE role gfeatures stayed 0 so features (=dfeatures&gfeatures) were 0
     and openvela's rpmsg_virtio.c:835 DEBUGASSERT(F_CPUNAME) tripped.
- Feature choice rationale: openvela NuttX rpmsg advertises a rich private set
  (F_NS|F_ACK|F_BUFSZ|F_CPUNAME|F_BUFADDR|F_PRIORITY) and hard-requires
  F_CPUNAME; mainline rockchip Linux rpmsg only offers F_NS. We advertise ONLY
  F_NS|F_CPUNAME: F_NS matches mainline (rpmsg_hdr + rpmsg_ns_msg are byte-
  identical to Linux), and F_CPUNAME only makes NuttX read cpu names from OUR
  local config (host_cpuname="linux", remote_cpuname="cpu3") -- it never goes
  on the wire. F_ACK/F_BUFADDR/F_PRIORITY are deliberately left off so the
  on-wire behaviour stays mainline-compatible.
- Resource-table config now sets config_len = sizeof(fw_rsc_config) and the
  host/remote cpu names.
- VERIFIED on target: boots clean, "[AMP] rptun init ok", "hb start", ticks +
  busy keep counting, NO assertion/panic anywhere in the log. Linux side:
  "rockchip-rpmsg ... vring0 0x7c00000 vring1 0x7c08000" + "virtio_rpmsg_bus
  virtio0: rpmsg host is online".
- REMAINING (app level): no rpmsg CHANNEL yet (/dev/rpmsg* empty). Linux (host)
  waits for the remote to announce a service, but nothing on the NuttX side
  creates an rpmsg endpoint, so no NS announcement is sent. NEXT: add a minimal
  NuttX rpmsg server (create a named endpoint on rpmsg-device-ready -> sends NS
  to Linux) and test round-trip via Linux rpmsg_char (/dev/rpmsg_ctrl0).


### rpmsg NuttX->Linux CHANNEL CREATED — one-way rpmsg works (VERIFIED)
- After the transport came up, the NS announcement still never reached Linux.
  Register dump (Linux devmem of mailbox0 @0xfec60000) was decisive:
    B2A_INTEN(0x28)=0x9  -> Linux DID enable B2A ch0 (+ch3) RX interrupts
    B2A_CMD0(0x30)=0x03, B2A_DAT0(0x34)=0x524D5347 -> NuttX's writes landed
    B2A_STATUS(0x2c)=0    -> but the status/trigger bit never set => no IRQ
    /proc/interrupts mailbox (SPI 61-64 = irq 93-96) all 0 => Linux never kicked
- Root causes found & fixed:
  1. STARTUP RACE: we pin DRIVER_OK so NuttX creates the rpmsg device and
     announces at ~0.5s, but Linux does not probe its mailbox / fill vrings /
     enable B2A_INTEN until ~3.4s. The single early doorbell was lost. Fix:
     defer the announce (echo_announce_thread sleeps 6s).
  2. SINGLE DOORBELL UNRELIABLE: even after Linux is up, a single B2A_CMD/DAT
     write did not reliably set B2A_STATUS (the very first kick failed; the
     manual heartbeat only ever worked because it repeated every second). Fix:
     re-announce the NS once per second (rpmsg_send_ns_message) until bound.
- Board bringup now runs a minimal rpmsg echo server (evb7_amp_bringup.c):
  device-created -> spawn echo_announce_thread -> sleep 6s -> rpmsg_create_ept
  "rpmsg-echo" -> re-announce loop. echo_ept_cb echoes RX data back.
- Also added VIRTIO_RING_F_MUST_NOTIFY (bit30) to the resource-table
  dfeatures/gfeatures: OpenAMP's vq_ring_must_notify() otherwise suppresses the
  doorbell when Linux's vring avail flags say NO_INTERRUPT; MUST_NOTIFY forces
  an unconditional kick (local flag, off-wire).
- VERIFIED on target (log @11:32): Linux dmesg
    "virtio_rpmsg_bus virtio0: creating channel rpmsg-echo addr 0x400"
    then "channel rpmsg-echo:ffffffff:400 already exist" (stable), and
    /sys/bus/rpmsg/devices/ shows "virtio0.rpmsg-echo.-1.1024".
  => NuttX cpu_l3 -> Linux rpmsg (NS + vring + mailbox) fully works one-way.
- STILL TODO: (a) reverse path Linux->NuttX: Linux sends on rpmsg-tx =
  mailbox0 channel 3 (A2B ch3), but our ISR enables/checks A2B ch0 -- must
  handle A2B ch3 so echo_ept_cb fires. (b) round-trip echo test from Linux via
  rpmsg_char (/dev/rpmsg_ctrl0). (c) strip temporary [RP] debug traces; reduce
  re-announce noise. (d) commit once bidirectional verified.


### rpmsg BIDIRECTIONAL WORKING — NuttX(cpu_l3) <-> Linux (VERIFIED, Task 10 done)
- Reverse path (Linux->NuttX) root cause: NuttX's arm64_gic_irq_enable() sets
  GICD_IROUTER = up_cpu_index() for SPIs; in our UP build up_cpu_index()=0, so
  mailbox A2B SPI 100 was routed to cpu0 (Linux), not cpu_l3. Writing IROUTER
  = 0x300 (cpu_l3 aff, matches dts CPU_GET_AFFINITY(3,0)=3<<8) still did not
  deliver -- rockchip AMP irq delivery also depends on GIC group/security state
  set up via BL31, which our bare NuttX GIC bring-up does not replicate.
- SOLUTION (works): poll A2B_STATUS in a NuttX kthread (rk3588_rptun_rx_poll,
  5ms) instead of relying on the SPI. Linux reliably sets A2B_STATUS (confirmed
  devmem=0x8 on ch3); the poll drains CMD/DAT, clears the bit, and calls the
  rptun callback -> OpenAMP processes the rvq. Bypasses the GIC routing issue.
- Channel names: NuttX announces "rpmsg-ap3-ch0" to match Linux
  rockchip_rpmsg_test id_table so that driver binds and runs a ping-pong.
- Linux side test driver built as a module (was not in the kernel):
  set CONFIG_RPMSG_ROCKCHIP_TEST=m in /media/1t/openvela/kernel/.config,
  built drivers/rpmsg/rockchip_rpmsg_test.ko (vermagic 6.1.141 SMP aarch64),
  copied to rk3588-amp-demo/. On target: insmod it (RPMSG_CHAR/CTRL are not
  built so /dev/rpmsg is unavailable; the rockchip test driver is the tester).
- VERIFIED on target (log @12:03): continuous ping-pong, Linux
  "rockchip_rpmsg_test ... rx msg Rockchip rpmsg linux test pingpong!
   rx_count 5697 (remote_ept_id: 0x400)"; NuttX rx-poll fired 5701x, echoed
  5697x. Fully bidirectional and stable.
- Mailbox channel map (rk3588-amp.dtsi mboxes=<&mailbox0 0 &mailbox0 3>):
  TX cpu_l3->Linux = B2A ch0 (Linux rpmsg-rx); RX Linux->cpu_l3 = A2B ch3
  (Linux rpmsg-tx). Single B2A doorbell writes are unreliable so NuttX
  re-announces the NS ~1/s until bound.
- TODO before commit: strip temporary [RP]/[MP] debug UART traces and the
  per-message prints (they spam the shared console ~5700x); keep the poll,
  re-announce, echo server. Then commit work/nuttx + demo + changelog.


### rpmsg STRESS-STABLE — stale-vring wipe + RT-inline echo (VERIFIED, Task 10 FINAL)
- The 5697 ping-pong above only survived because the echo path had a slow UART
  print throttling it. With the print removed, the rockchip test driver's tight
  echo crashed cpu_l3 with TWO different asserts across iterations:
    1. rpmsg/rpmsg.c:1052  DEBUGASSERT(work != NULL) in rpmsg_queue_rx_work
       (task rptun_rx) — the deferred RX work pool (freerx) exhausted.
    2. open-amp/lib/rpmsg/rpmsg_virtio.c:900 RPMSG_ASSERT(BUF_HELD_COUNTER>0)
       (task rpmsg-linux-0) — a buffer's held ref-count underflowed.
- Root-caused with two temporary syslog probes ([RXD]) in the RX dispatcher
  (print nrx + the dst/src/len/ept/prio/last of the first buffers). They showed:
    * the echo data message (dst=1024, len=51) matched ept=NULL at first because
      it was consumed BEFORE our endpoint existed;
    * the SAME name-service buffer (dst=53) was dispatched 20+ times in a row.
- TWO independent bugs, previously masking each other:
  1. STALE VRING: the vrings live in reserved DDR (0x07c00000) which survives a
     soft reset. NuttX pins DRIVER_OK and starts consuming long before Linux
     re-initialises the rings, so it read a stale avail->idx left by the PREVIOUS
     run and replayed dozens of orphaned descriptors -> freerx exhaustion / held
     underflow. Proof: a dst=1024 len=51 echo payload appeared in the ring before
     this boot's Linux `new channel`, i.e. it was left over from a prior session.
     FIX (rk3588_rptun.c): memset-clear the vring desc/avail/used areas at
     rk3588_rptun_init() before any vring access (vring0 @0x07c00000 full 0x8000
     slot; vring1 @0x07c08000 up to the resource table at 0x07c0f000). Cold-boot
     safe: Linux re-inits its rings ~15s later.
  2. DEFERRED-PATH OVERLOAD: echo RX went through the work-queue (freerx) path.
     FIX (evb7_amp_bringup.c): set the echo endpoint to RPMSG_PRIO_RT and set it
     BEFORE rpmsg_create_ept (mainline rpmsg_ping/socket ordering). At RT the
     dispatcher (rpmsg_virtio_rx_dispatch) processes the callback INLINE on the
     poll thread and never consumes an RX work item. echo_ept_cb uses the
     non-blocking rpmsg_trysend so a momentary TX-full just drops one echo
     (self-throttle) instead of stalling.
- The earlier `nrx *= 4` patch in the shared drivers/rpmsg/rpmsg_virtio.c was a
  band-aid; it is REVERTED. The real bug was the stale vring, so the shared
  OpenAMP/rpmsg drivers are now byte-for-byte upstream (git diff clean).
- VERIFIED on target (clean production build, all [RXD]/diagnostic prints
  removed): NO assert anywhere; Linux `rockchip_rpmsg_test ... rx_count` climbs
  continuously (observed 1584 and, in the diagnostic run, 3038) at a steady
  ~6ms cadence; the stale NS replay dropped from 20+ to a single buffer;
  echo_ept_cb confirmed running on tid=5 (rptun_rx poll thread) = RT inline path.
- Files (all in external work/nuttx tree, committed there):
    arch/arm64/src/rk3588/rk3588_rptun.c        (+ stale-vring wipe)
    boards/arm64/rk3588/evb7-amp/src/evb7_amp_bringup.c (RT-inline echo server)
  Shared drivers/rpmsg/{rpmsg.c,rpmsg_virtio.c}: unchanged (nrx*=4 reverted).
- STATUS: NuttX(cpu_l3) <-> Linux rpmsg is bidirectional AND stress-stable.
  Task 10 COMPLETE.


## rpmsg RX 中断化尝试:改 OP-TEE 把 mailbox INTID 100 设 Group1NS (进行中,待硬件验证)

目标:把 Linux->NuttX 的 RX 从 5ms 轮询改成真中断。障碍是 mailbox A2B(GIC INTID 100
= SPI 68)被配成 **Group0(secure)**,NS-EL1 的 NuttX 收不到(Group0 走 FIQ->EL3)。

### 可行性调研结论(全部实测/源码确认)
- NS 侧(u-boot NS-EL2 / Linux / NuttX)都改不了 GIC 分组:
  * Linux devmem 写 IGROUPR[3] bit4=1 回读仍 0 (RAZ/WI)。
  * u-boot `CONFIG_SPL_ATF=y`+`ARMV8_PSCI` 未开 => NS-EL2,同样无权。
  * BL31 的 `SIP_ACCESS_REG`(0x82000002) SMC 有地址白名单:内核模块探针实测
    写 0xfe60008c 返回 a0=-4(拒绝),GICD 不在白名单。
    (探针工具 gicgrp_test 已删除;此路后被"RX 本就是 Group1NS"里程碑推翻)
- INTID 编号厘清:rk3588-amp.dtsi 的 amp-irqs 用 **GIC INTID**(UART5=368=SPI336+32
  佐证)。mailbox 那条=INTID 100=SPI 68=A2B ch3。NuttX 之前误用 100+32=132(SPI100)
  监听了无关中断,这是"中断从未到达"的根因之一。
- 主线 TF-A(已 clone work/tfa)分析:rk3588 plat 无 rockchip 私有 AMP SIP
  (plat_sip_calls 只有 SCMI);但主线 `gicv3_spis_config_defaults` 默认把所有 SPI
  设 Group1NS,rockchip secure props 不含 INTID 100 => 主线 TF-A 下 INTID 100 天然
  Group1NS。惟主线无 AMP 拉核(我们 its hyp=0 起 NS-EL1 依赖 vendor 私有 SIP_AMP_CFG)。
- 结论:唯一能"既保 vendor AMP 拉核、又把 INTID 100 改 Group1NS"的点是 **BL32/OP-TEE
  (secure EL1,能写 IGROUPR)**。OP-TEE 有源码。

### 本次改动(external 源码树,均可回退)
1. NuttX `arch/arm64/src/rk3588/rk3588_rptun.c`(未提交):
   - `RK3588_MBOX_A2B_IRQ` 132 -> **100**(真正的 A2B ch3 INTID)。
   - init 里 `up_enable_irq(100)` + 直接写 `GICD_IROUTER(100)=0x300`(cpu_l3),
     因为 NuttX 通用 gic enable 会写 IROUTER=up_cpu_index()=0(=cpu0)。
   - ISR 加 `[MBOXIRQ]` 诊断打印。轮询暂留作 fallback。
2. 主线 OP-TEE(clone 到 `/media/1t/openvela/work/optee`):
   - `core/arch/arm/plat-rockchip/main.c` `boot_primary_init_intc()`:gic_init 后,
     #if PLATFORM_FLAVOR_rk3588 用 phys_to_virt(GICD_BASE) 写
     `IGROUPR[3] |= BIT(4)` + `IGRPMODR[3] &= ~BIT(4)` => INTID 100 -> Group1NS。
   - 编译:`PLATFORM=rockchip PLATFORM_FLAVOR=rk3588 CFG_ARM64_core=y
     CFG_USER_TA_TARGETS=ta_arm64 CFG_DT=y CFG_CORE_ARM64_PA_BITS=40
     CFG_TZDRAM_START=0x08400000 CFG_TZDRAM_SIZE=0x00200000`
     (TZDRAM 必须=0x08400000,匹配 vendor BL32 加载地址且避开 NuttX 的 0x30000000;
     主线默认 0x30000000 会撞 NuttX)。产物 out/arm-plat-rockchip/core/tee.bin。
3. rkbin(改动已备份):
   - `cp tee.bin bin/rk35/rk3588_bl32_optee_gicpatch.bin`
   - `RKTRUST/RK3588TRUST.ini` 的 BL32 PATH 改指向该文件(BL31 仍 v1.54 不动)。
   - 备份:`RKTRUST/RK3588TRUST.ini.orig`。
4. u-boot:`./make.sh rk3588` 重新打包 `uboot.img`(FIT: vendor BL31 + patched OP-TEE
   @0x08400000 + u-boot)。dumpimage 确认 optee 段 Load 0x08400000。

### 备份 / 恢复(验证失败退回官方)
- `/media/1t/openvela/u-boot/_amp_backup/`:uboot.img.orig, tee.bin.orig, spl_loader。
- `/media/1t/openvela/rkbin/RKTRUST/RK3588TRUST.ini.orig`。
- 失败退回:MASKROM 下 `rkdeveloptool db rk3588_spl_loader_v1.21.114.bin;
  rkdeveloptool wlx uboot _amp_backup/uboot.img.orig; rkdeveloptool rd`。

### 待硬件验证
1. 刷新 uboot.img,板子能否正常启动(主线 OP-TEE + vendor BL31 兼容性,最大风险)。
2. Linux `busybox devmem 0xfe60008c 32` bit4 应=1(Group1NS)。
3. 刷中断版 amp.img,看 NuttX `[MBOXIRQ]` 是否触发、rx_count 是否正常。


### 硬件验证结果:主线 OP-TEE + vendor BL31 不兼容,启动卡死(第一步失败)
- 刷入 patched uboot.img 后,串口日志停在:
  `NOTICE: BL31: v2.3...fwver: v1.54` → `INFO: BL31: Initializing BL32` → **无后续**。
- 主线 OP-TEE 4.x 连第一行 `I/TC:` 都没打印就挂 => 在 OP-TEE entry/handoff 极早期
  fault。确认 **vendor BL31 v1.54 与主线 OP-TEE 4.x 的 BL31<->BL32 交接协议不兼容**
  (boot args / DTB / 共享内存 / entry 约定不一致)。到不了 Linux。
- 处置:恢复。rkbin `RK3588TRUST.ini` 已改回原始 BL32(v1.20);uboot 分区用
  `_amp_backup/uboot.img.orig` 刷回官方,板子恢复正常。
- 结论:OP-TEE 改 GIC 分组这条路**思路成立且代码就绪**(main.c patch + 编译 OK +
  optee 段正确打进 uboot.img @0x08400000),但**必须用 vendor OP-TEE 源码
  (external_optee_optee_os,与 v1.20 同源)**才能与 vendor BL31 兼容启动;主线 OP-TEE
  混搭不可行。
- 留档:patch 后的主线 OP-TEE 在 work/optee;NuttX 中断版(INTID 100)在 work/nuttx
  (未提交);gicgrp_test.ko 探针已删除。
- 现状:RX 仍用已提交的稳定**轮询**方案。中断化待 vendor OP-TEE 源码到位后再试。


### 最终决定:保留中断路径代码 + 当前用轮询 (已提交)
- OP-TEE 改 group 的思路/代码已验证就绪,但受阻于 vendor BL31↔主线 OP-TEE 不兼容
  (需 vendor OP-TEE 源码 external_optee_optee_os,暂缺)。决定先用轮询交付。
- NuttX `rk3588_rptun.c` 保留完整中断路径(INTID 100 + up_enable_irq + IROUTER=0x300
  修正 + ISR 实际处理),并保留 5ms 轮询线程作 fallback;清掉了 [MBOXIRQ] 调试打印。
  当前 vendor OP-TEE 把 INTID 100 留在 Group0,中断进不来,自动走轮询;将来 secure 侧
  把它改成 Group1NS 后,ISR 无需改动即接管。
- 已提交 work/nuttx: **e48555b2210** "rk3588: add mailbox A2B RX interrupt path
  (falls back to polling)" (仅 rk3588_rptun.c,+32/-5,带 Signed-off-by)。
- 硬件验证(官方 OP-TEE 下):boots、rptun init ok、echo announced、无 [MBOXIRQ]
  (符合预期)、rx_count 涨到 2741、无 assert => 轮询 fallback 正常。
- 环境恢复:主线 OP-TEE/TF-A clone 已删;u-boot uboot.img/tee.bin 及 rkbin ini 已还原
  官方;板子刷回官方 uboot.img。gicgrp_test.ko 探针已删除。
- 重开中断的路径(future):取 external_optee_optee_os → 移植 main.c 那两行
  IGROUPR/IGRPMODR patch (INTID 100 -> Group1NS) → 编 bl32 替换 rkbin → 重打包 uboot.img。


## 调查记录：RK3588 内置 MCU / PMU 现状（无代码改动，纯排查留档）

### 背景
排查 RK3588 除 8 个 A 核外的内置协处理器是否可用于 AMP，尤其是能否绕开 A55
上 GIC Group0/secure 收不到 mailbox 中断（INTID 100）的死结。

### datasheet（Rockchip_RK3588_Datasheet_V1.6-20231016.pdf）关键事实
- RK3588 内含**三颗 Cortex-M0 MCU**（§1.2.3 System Component）：
  | MCU | 域 | 片内存储 | 用途 |
  |-----|-----|---------|------|
  | PMU_M0 | VD_PMU | 16KB Cache + 16KB TCM | 电源管理（可编程，当前空闲） |
  | NPU_M0 | VD_NPU | 16KB Cache + 64KB TCM | NPU 控制 |
  | DDR_M0 | PD_CENTER | 32KB TCM | DDR 管理（闭源黑盒，每次开机必用） |
- **关键原文**："Integrated Programmable Interrupt Controller, all IRQ lines
  connected to GIC for CPU also connect to MCU in VD_PMU(PMU_M0) and
  PD_CENTER(DDR_M0)" —— 即 INTMUX 把接到 GIC 的所有中断线**同时**也接到
  PMU_M0/DDR_M0。→ M0 收中断走 INTMUX→NVIC，**完全绕开 GIC 的 Group0/secure
  分组**，天生没有 A55 上那个死结。
- SoC 有 **3 个 Mailbox** 专供 CPU↔MCU 通信（rpmsg 现用 mailbox0）。
- PMU：10 个电压域、45 个电源域、软件可逐个上下电、VD_PMU 内含 PMU SRAM。
- 注意：datasheet 为纯功能/电气文档，**无任何寄存器地址映射**（0 个 hex 基址）；
  INTMUX/boot-addr 的寄存器位定义只有 **TRM** 才有。

### PMU_CM0 当前未使用 —— 7 条证据全部闭合（确证空闲）
| # | 检查 | 结果 | 判定 |
|---|------|------|------|
| 1 | AMP FIT 目标核 (amp-nuttx.its) | arch=arm64, cpu=0x300 | remote 是 A55，非 M0 |
| 2 | rkbin RK3588 M0 固件 | 不存在（仅 3528/3562/3576 有 mcu.bin） | 无镜像可加载 |
| 3 | loader/trust ini (RK3588MINIALL/RK3588TRUST) | grep cm0/mcu 零命中 | boot 链不加载 M0 |
| 4 | u-boot M0 启动代码 | 无匹配 | 无人解复位 |
| 5 | rk3588-amp.dtsi rpmsg 链路 | 仅 1 条 link-id=0x03 → A55 | 通信无 M0 |
| 6 | mcu@0x7a00000 首字 (devmem) | 0xFBFFFFFF（非对齐垃圾值） | 保留区无有效固件 |
| 7 | PMU1SOFTRST_CON00 bit13 (devmem 0xFD7F0A00) | 0x00002000（bit13=1） | M0 core 被摁在复位 |

### 实测寄存器地址清单（来自 kernel/u-boot dts+cru 头，datasheet 无）
- CRU 基址：`0xfd7c0000`（`rockchip,rk3588-cru`，size 0x5c000）
- **PMU_M0 core 复位**：PMU1SOFTRST_CON00 @ 偏移 0x30A00 → 绝对
  `0xFD7F0A00`，bit13 (SRST_F_PMU_CM0_CORE=786445)。1=复位/停，0=释放/可跑。
- PMUCM0_INTMUX 复位：SOFTRST_CON19 @ 0xA4C → `0xfd7c0a4c` bit4
  (SRST_P_PMUCM0_INTMUX=308)
- INTMUX 时钟门：PCLK_PMUCM0_INTMUX（CLK_IS_CRITICAL，常开）
- mailbox0：`0xfec60000`（mailbox1 0xfec70000 / mailbox2 0xfece0000）
- pmu0_grf：`0xfd588000`；pmu1_grf：`0xfd58a000`（PMU_M0 boot-addr 寄存器很可能
  在此，精确偏移待 TRM）；sys_grf：`0xfd58c000`；php_grf：`0xfd5b0000`

### 结论
- 当前 AMP 只用一颗 A55(cpu_l3) 跑 NuttX；PMU_CM0 空闲、复位、内存空、无固件、
  无 rpmsg。DDR_M0 是不可编程黑盒（DDR init/训练/DVFS）。NPU_M0 专用于 NPU。
- 若将来做三层异构：PMU_M0 是现成空位，且其中断经 INTMUX→NVIC 绕开 GIC 安全域，
  可真正以中断方式收 mailbox（解掉 A55 轮询的根因）。代价：需单独一份 ARMv6-M
  (Thumb) 固件、查 TRM 的 INTMUX/boot-addr 寄存器、boot 链加一段把固件搬进
  mcu_reserved(0x7a00000) 并解复位、Linux 侧加一条 rpmsg link。参考模板：RK3506/
  RK3576 官方 M0 AMP 例子（同 rockchip,amp 框架）。


### 补充：M0 主频 + RK3588 vs RK3576 AMP 示例对比（延续上节调查）

#### PMU_CM0 主频
- M0 core 时钟 `FCLK_PMU_CM0_CORE` ← `hclk_pmu_cm0_root`（四选一 mux，NODIV）：
  源可选 `clk_pmu1_400m_src / 200m / 100m / xin24m`。**硬件上限 400 MHz。**
- 当前档位（只读实测）：`devmem 0xFD7F0304`（PMU_CLKSEL_CON1）= `0x00000400`
  → bit[11:10]=`0b01` → 选 `clk_pmu1_200m_src` → **当前配置 200 MHz**（M0 仍复位，
  此为解复位后会跑的档位）。
- 画像：Cortex-M0 @ 200MHz(上限400)、ARMv6-M、无 FPU、16KB Cache + 16KB TCM。

#### RK3588 只有大核 AMP 示例；RK3576 参考里 M0 是一等公民
核实内核 dts（非凭印象）：
| dts | link-id | remote 核 | M0 情况 |
|-----|---------|-----------|---------|
| rk3588-amp.dtsi | 0x03 | CPU3 (A55) | 仅保留 mcu@7a00000，无 M0 link/示例 |
| rk3588-robotic-evb-v11-amp.dtsi | 0x03 | CPU3（`/delete-node/ &cpu_l3`） | 同上，无 M0 |
| rk3576-amp.dtsi | 0x03(激活) | CPU3 | **注释显式列出 `MCU: link-id 0x04`**；mailbox0+mailbox3 双通道 |
| rk3576-vehicle-amp.dtsi | 0x03(激活) | CPU3 | 同样有 `MCU: link-id 0x04` 注释 |

- **结论**：RK3588 官方 AMP 参考 = **仅 A 核**（M0 只占保留内存，无 rpmsg link、无示例）。
  RK3576 的 AMP 参考**把 M0 作为正式 rpmsg 端点列出**（link-id 0x04 + 双 mailbox），
  Linux 侧集成钩子齐备——比 RK3588 更进一步。
- **重要澄清**：以上均为 **Linux 侧集成**（dts/link/mailbox）。真正跑在 M0 上的固件
  （startup+rpmsg+INTMUX 配置）在 Rockchip RTOS/HAL/bare-metal SDK 里，不在此 Linux 树；
  公开镜像未见 RK3576 M0 固件源码，需走 Rockchip 官方/合作伙伴 SDK。
- **对 RK3588 M0 移植的价值**：**RK3576 是最佳模板**——同 rockchip,amp 框架、同
  CM0+INTMUX+mailbox 结构，且 RK3576 已把 M0 的 link-id(0x04)/mailbox/内存布局规划好。
  拿 RK3576 M0 SDK 固件 + link 0x04 dts 配置，改成 RK3588 寄存器基址，是最省力路径。


## 里程碑:rpmsg RX 改为真·中断驱动(纯 NS 侧,推翻早前 Group0 结论)

### 结论(TL;DR)
- **mailbox A2B RX 中断(INTID 100)本就是 Non-secure Group-1,能正常投递到 NS-EL1 的 cpu_l3。** 早前"Group0 收不到、必须改 BL31/OP-TEE 或换 M0"的结论**是错的**。
- 真凶:**Linux `gic_dist_init`(~15s)复位共享 GICD 时禁用所有 SPI(ICENABLER)、且 rockchip-amp 只重写 IROUTER 不重新使能;Linux 还清掉了 mailbox 的 A2B_INTEN**。于是 SPI 被关 + INTEN 被清,NuttX 开机只使能过一次、没再武装 → 中断进不来 → 之前误判成 Group0。
- **修复(纯 NS,不碰 BL31/OP-TEE,不用 M0)**:NuttX 用一个低频 keepalive 线程周期性重写 **GICD_ISENABLER(100) + IROUTER(100)=cpu_l3 + MBOX A2B_INTEN(RX 通道)**。RX 交给 ISR。→ RX 全程中断驱动。

### 验证(target 实测)
- keepalive 生效后,把轮询线程改成"只重新使能、不再收 RX",rpmsg pingpong **rx_count 一路涨到 10000**、`[RXIRQ 100]` 9870 次、**0 assert** → 数据只可能是 ISR 收的 = 真·中断驱动坐实。
- 手动 `devmem` 戳 A2B 任一通道(需 root + 先写 CMD 触发)均能触发 ISR:`[RXIRQ 97/99/100]` 都出现 → **4 条 A2B(INTID 97-100)全是 Group1NS,都能到 NS**(不止 ch3)。

### 早前误判的方法论教训(重要,避免再踩)
1. **NS 侧读 `GICD_IGROUPR`/`IGRPMODR` 是 RAZ(恒读 0)**,DS=0(有安全世界)时对 secure 中断更是如此。**不能用"读出来是 0"判定 Group0**——早前正是把 RAZ 的 0 当成 Group0,是错误链条的起点。判组只能靠"行为(能否收)"或安全侧读。
2. **判"中断收不到"必须先排除"被对端 OS 禁用"**:Linux 会 disable 共享 GICD 的 SPI + 清 mailbox INTEN。不持续重新使能就测,会把"被 Linux 关了"误判成"分组锁死"。
3. **devmem 触发要点**:需 root;且要**先写 A2B_CMD 才触发中断**(写 DAT 不触发)。非 root / 只写 DAT 会得到"无中断"的假阴性。
4. IROUTER(100) 早前注释说被 revert 到 0x500,实测其实是 0x300(cpu_l3),路由一直正常;卡点从来是"被禁用",不是路由也不是分组。

### 代码改动(work/nuttx,external tree,未提交)
- `arch/arm64/src/rk3588/rk3588_rptun.c`:
  - ISR `rk3588_rptun_isr` 成为唯一 RX 路径(读 A2B_STATUS、清 pending、notify OpenAMP)。
  - 新增 keepalive 线程 `rk3588_rptun_irq_keepalive`(100ms):重写 ISENABLER(100)+IROUTER(100)=0x300+A2B_INTEN(RX 通道)。替换原 5ms 轮询收包线程。
  - init:只 attach/enable/route INTID 100 + 使能 A2B RX 通道的 mbox 中断。
  - 更新顶部注释(删掉"Group0/只能轮询"的错误叙述)。
  - 新增宏 `RK3588_GICD_ISENABLER(n)` / `_BIT(n)`。
- 关键地址速查:GICD=0xfe600000;ISENABLER3=0xfe60010c(INTID100=bit4=0x10);IROUTER(100)=0xfe606320;mailbox0=0xfec60000(A2B_CMD(3)=+0x20, A2B_DAT(3)=+0x24, A2B_INTEN=+0x00)。

### 状态
- amp.img 已重编打包(352KB)。待烧录确认干净版仍中断驱动(rx_count 涨即可)。
- 待提交:work/nuttx 的 rk3588_rptun.c(带 -s)。此前的中断/轮询提交(e48555b2210)将被这版取代。


### 最终方案定型 + 验证通过(正式中断驱动版)
- **RX = 纯 ISR 中断驱动**(`rk3588_rptun_isr` 读 A2B_STATUS→清 pending→notify OpenAMP)。
- **保活 = 有界 keepalive**:`rk3588_rptun_irq_keepalive` 头 30s 每 100ms 调 `rk3588_rptun_irq_rearm()`(重写 GICD_ISENABLER(100)+IROUTER(100)=0x300+A2B_INTEN(RX 通道)),熬过 Linux 一次性 `gic_dist_init`(~15s)后**线程退出**,稳态零轮询。
- **TX backstop = 已注释保留**:`rk3588_rptun_notify` 里 `rk3588_rptun_irq_rearm()` 注释掉,带 `TODO(suspend/resume)` 说明——本板无休眠/hotplug 场景,启动 keepalive 已足够;将来引入 Linux S3/hotplug 再打开(否则 resume 后中断会失效无人重开)。
- **验证(target,正式版那次启动)**:输出无 `[RXIRQ]` 调试串、rpmsg pingpong `rx_count → 10000`、**0 assert**,且跑到 ~46s(keepalive 30s 已退出)RX 仍正常 → 稳态纯中断驱动确认。
- 关键参数:INTID 100(A2B ch3);ISENABLER3=0xFE60010C bit4;IROUTER(100)=0xFE606320=0x300;A2B_INTEN=0xFEC60000 bit3;keepalive 300×100ms=30s。
- 取代早前的轮询/中断混合提交(e48555b2210)。

- 已提交 work/nuttx: **ef649eb63f1** "rk3588: interrupt-driven rpmsg RX via mailbox A2B (INTID 100)"(仅 rk3588_rptun.c,+94/-66,带 Signed-off-by chenjinglin）。evb7_amp_bringup.c 的去刷屏改动属另一件事,未随本次提交。


### 收尾：提交 bringup 去刷屏 + 删除弯路探针工具
- **work/nuttx** 提交 `e77643513cd` "evb7-amp: drop debug heartbeat/busy threads
  and manual mailbox kick"(仅 evb7_amp_bringup.c,+3/-110)：删掉 amp_heartbeat/
  amp_busy/amp_putu 三个调试线程 + 手动 B2A_CMD/DAT mailbox kick，只留 rpmsg echo
  server + 少量 [AMP] 状态行。这是之前 changelog 提到"未随 ef649eb63f1 提交"的那件事。
  真机验证：rpmsg pingpong rx_count→10000、0 assert、无刷屏。
- **删除 gicgrp_test 探针工具**(rk3588-amp-demo/gicgrp_test/)：这是"借 BL31 SIP_ACCESS_REG
  从 NS 侧改 GIC 分组"那条弯路的探路内核模块，结论(BL31 白名单拒绝 GICD)虽真实，但整条
  路线已被"RX 本就是 Group1NS、真凶是 Linux 禁用 SPI"的最终里程碑推翻。正确结论已在
  changelog 里，故删除工具本体；本文档中相关"留档位置"引用同步更新为"已删除"。


## UART 共用：一根 UART2 线上在 Linux / NuttX nsh 间切换（rpmsg-tty，路线 Z）

目标：不接第二根串口线,在现有 UART2 调试线上,既能用 Linux shell,又能进
cpu_l3 上 NuttX 的交互式 nsh。做法是把 NuttX 的 console 通过 rpmsg 隧道到 Linux
(逻辑复用,非物理 UART 分时独占)。选型理由:物理 UART2 分时独占要和 Linux 主
console 抢 8250,脆弱;rpmsg 通道已通,复用最稳。

**路线 Z**：NuttX 侧用已验证的现成 `uart_rpmsg.c`(零新代码,一行 init);Linux 侧
新写配套 tty 驱动解析 NuttX 的 uart_rpmsg header 协议。把工作量放在 Linux 侧
(更熟、崩了不影响 AMP 核),NuttX 那侧(最脆弱)不动。

### 改动清单
| # | 文件 | 类型 | 改动 | 为什么 |
|---|------|------|------|--------|
| 1 | work/nuttx `boards/arm64/rk3588/evb7-amp/configs/nsh/defconfig` | 其他(NuttX config) | 加 `CONFIG_RPMSG_UART=y`(RPMSG_CHAR 后、RPTUN 前) | 启用 uart_rpmsg 虚拟串口;自动 select SERIAL_RXDMA/TXDMA/TERMIOS/REMOVABLE/IFLOWCONTROL |
| 2 | work/nuttx `.../src/evb7_amp_bringup.c` | 其他(NuttX board) | include `<nuttx/serial/uart_rpmsg.h>`;rptun init 前加 `uart_rpmsg_init("linux","proxy",4096,true)` | 建 /dev/console+/dev/ttyproxy + endpoint "rpmsg-ttyproxy";cpuname "linux" 匹配 rptun remote 名;isconsole=true 让 nsh_main 用它当交互 console |
| 3 | kernel `drivers/tty/rpmsg_nsh_tty.c` | kernel-driver(新) | 新写 Linux 配套 tty 驱动 | 解析 NuttX uart_rpmsg header 协议(标准 rpmsg_tty 是裸协议不兼容),把 NuttX nsh 暴露成 /dev/ttyNSHx |
| 4 | kernel `drivers/tty/Kconfig` | kernel-config | 加 `config RPMSG_NSH_TTY`(tristate, depends RPMSG) | 新驱动的开关 |
| 5 | kernel `drivers/tty/Makefile` | kernel-config | 加 `obj-$(CONFIG_RPMSG_NSH_TTY) += rpmsg_nsh_tty.o` | 挂进构建 |

### 关键设计点
- **协议**：NuttX `uart_rpmsg` 每包带 header `{command:31+response:1; result; cookie}`(16B)
  + `{count; resolved; data[]}`(共 24B+data),单包 payload ≤496B。这跟上游
  `rpmsg_tty.c` 的裸字节协议**不兼容**,所以必须写配套驱动。
- **设备名 ttyNSH**（非 ttyRPMSG）：内核 `.config` 里 `CONFIG_RPMSG_TTY=y` 已占用
  ttyRPMSG,故新驱动用独立名 ttyNSH,两者可共存。endpoint 名 "rpmsg-ttyproxy"
  (= UART_RPMSG_EPT_PREFIX "rpmsg-tty" + devname "proxy")。
- **RX/TX**：NuttX→Linux 的 TTY_WRITE 塞进 tty 后就地改 header(response=1,
  result=收下字节数)回发做流控 ack;Linux→NuttX 的用户输入包成 TTY_WRITE 用
  `rpmsg_trysend`(满了不阻塞)。TTY_WAKEUP 当前 no-op(靠 trysend 背压)。

### 编译验证（已做）
- Linux 驱动:`make ARCH=arm64 CROSS_COMPILE=aarch64-linux-gnu- drivers/tty/rpmsg_nsh_tty.o`
  **通过,无 warning/error**,生成 ~49KB .o。
- 编译时 `.config` 设了 `CONFIG_RPMSG_NSH_TTY=m`(olddefconfig)。`.config` 是生成物,
  **armbian 正式构建须把此开关加进 kernel config fragment** 才不会丢。

### 待真机验证 / 风险项（未上板）
- NuttX 侧改动**尚未编译**(需 openvela 交叉工具链编 NuttX 固件 + 重打包 amp.img)。
- isconsole=true 把 /dev/console 换成 rpmsg tty:早期 syslog(rpmsg ready 前)可能丢;
  但 [AMP] 早期日志走 amp_puts(polled UART2)不受影响。nsh_main 打开 rpmsg console
  在 Linux 未连时 read 阻塞(预期行为),需真机确认 nsh 能正常起。
- 时序:uart_rpmsg_init 在 board_late_initialize,先于 INIT_ENTRYPOINT(nsh_main),
  设备节点应已就绪。rpmsg 通道要等 Linux 探测才通(依赖已完成的 RX 中断化)。
- 首帧/欢迎语可能丢(rpmsg 未 ready 时 dmasend 丢弃);连上后每次回车会重画 prompt。

### 使用方式（真机）
Linux 侧加载模块后:`microcom -s 115200 /dev/ttyNSH0` 进 NuttX nsh,`Ctrl-]` 退出回
Linux。虚拟 tty 无真实波特率,-s 仅占位。


### UART 共用：编译验证 + 关键修正（rpmsg_serialinit）
- **根因修正**：最初把 `uart_rpmsg_init()` 放在 `evb7_amp_bringup()` 里,编译报
  `undefined reference to 'rpmsg_serialinit'`(drivers/drivers_initialize.c:211)。
  NuttX 在 `CONFIG_RPMSG_UART=y` 时,`drivers_initialize()` 会**自动调用 board 提供的
  `rpmsg_serialinit(void)`**(声明在 include/nuttx/drivers/drivers.h,mx8mp verdin 同款)。
  已把 uart_rpmsg_init 从 bringup 移出,改为在 `evb7_amp_bringup.c` 里定义:
  `void rpmsg_serialinit(void){ uart_rpmsg_init("linux","proxy",4096,true); }`。
  它在驱动初始化阶段(早于 board_late_initialize)运行,端点回调登记更早,更稳。
- **配置生效前提**：改 `configs/nsh/defconfig` 后**必须 `make distclean` + `configure.sh`**
  重新生成 .config,否则旧 .config 里 `# CONFIG_RPMSG_UART is not set` 不会变。
  重配后确认 .config 已有 CONFIG_RPMSG_UART=y + SERIAL_RXDMA/TXDMA/TERMIOS/REMOVABLE/IFLOWCONTROL。
- **编译验证(已过)**：NuttX `make -j4` 通过,`LD: nuttx` + `CP: nuttx.bin`;固件 strings
  含 "rpmsg-tty"(UART_RPMSG_EPT_PREFIX),确认 uart_rpmsg 已链接进去。
- **已打包 amp.img**：nuttx.bin(18:20) → mkimage amp-nuttx.its → amp.img(Load 0x30000000,
  AArch64)。**待刷机**:`rkdeveloptool db ... && wlx amp amp.img && rd`,重启。
- **首次真机验证(旧固件)复盘**：日志证实之前板子跑的是 7/13 旧固件(只有 rpmsg-ap3-ch0,
  无 rpmsg-ttyproxy),且 insmod 了模块但 /dev/ttyNSH0 不存在——正是因为 NuttX 侧没重编。
  现新固件已就绪,刷机后应出现 rpmsg-ttyproxy 通道 → 模块 probe → /dev/ttyNSH0。


### UART 共用：第二次真机复盘 + 根因修复（announce 时机 / re-announce）
- **真机现象**：刷入带标记的新固件后,日志确认 `[AMP] build=nsh-over-rpmsg` +
  `[AMP] rpmsg_serialinit: uart_rpmsg proxy` **都出现了**(新固件+rpmsg_serialinit 生效),
  但 Linux 侧仍无 `rpmsg-ttyproxy` 通道,`/dev/ttyNSH0` 不存在。
- **根因(时序)**:日志时间线——`290 rpmsg device ready`(NuttX 侧 <1s,因 rptun pin
  DRIVER_OK 早) vs `3.45s rpmsg host is online`(Linux 侧才就绪)。uart_rpmsg 在
  device-created(290,早期)就 `rpmsg_create_ept` 发 NS announce,那时 Linux 没 online、
  NuttX TX vring 还没被填充,announce 拿不到 buffer 丢失;而 `uart_rpmsg.c` **只 announce
  一次、不重试**,所以 Linux 永远收不到 → 无通道 → 无 /dev/ttyNSH0。
  echo 之所以成功:它 `sleep(6)` 等 Linux + re-announce 30 次(正是早先记的教训),
  uart_rpmsg 没有。
- **为何不能用"延迟 uart_rpmsg_init"绕过**:`nsh_consolemain` 无等待/重试 console 逻辑,
  `/dev/console` 必须早注册,否则 nsh 的 stdio 绑不上。所以必须保持 uart_rpmsg_init 早
  调用,只延迟 announce。
- **修复(改通用驱动 `nuttx/drivers/serial/uart_rpmsg.c`)**:device_created 里 create_ept
  之后,用 HPWORK 定时 re-announce(每 1s 一次,最多 30 次)直到 `ept.dest_addr` 被
  master 绑定;device_destroy 里 work_cancel。priv 结构加 `announce_work`/`announce_cnt`。
  对其他板子无害(master 早 online 时首个 announce 即 bound,work 立即退出)。
  rpmsg_send_ns_message/RPMSG_NS_CREATE/is_rpmsg_ept_ready 经 nuttx/rpmsg/rpmsg.h 已可达。
- **编译+打包(已过)**:NuttX make 通过(CC uart_rpmsg.c + LD nuttx);amp.img 重打包
  18:44,Load 0x30000000,hash 071a309f。**待刷机再验证**。
- 新增待提交文件:`work/nuttx/drivers/serial/uart_rpmsg.c`(通用驱动改动)。


### UART 共用：第三次真机复盘 + 根因（注册相位）
- **真机现象(v2 固件)**:`[AMP] build=nsh-over-rpmsg v2-reannounce` + `rpmsg_serialinit`
  都出现(固件确对),但**没有** `[uart_rpmsg] device_created: ept armed`、没有 `reannounce
  sent`,Linux 仍无 rpmsg-ttyproxy。→ uart_rpmsg 的 **device-created 回调根本没触发**。
- **诊断手段**:确认 `arm64_lowputc` 写 UART2(物理口),且 syslog 无其它 channel → NuttX
  `syslog()` 直接输出到物理日志可见。据此在 uart_rpmsg 埋了 syslog,精确定位到"回调没触发"。
- **根因(注册相位)**:CONFIG_SMP 未开(单核),排除 per-CPU。uart_rpmsg 在 `rpmsg_serialinit`
  (drivers_initialize 相位)调 rpmsg_register_callback,但该相位注册的 callback 在
  rk3588_rptun_init 创建 rpmsg device 时**未被 rpmsg_device_created 遍历到**;而 echo 在
  `evb7_amp_bringup`(board_late_initialize 相位)注册就正常触发。
- **修复**:把 `uart_rpmsg_init("linux","proxy",4096,true)` 从 rpmsg_serialinit **挪到
  evb7_amp_bringup**(echo 注册之后、rk3588_rptun_init 之前,同相位)。rpmsg_serialinit 保留
  为空壳(drivers_initialize 仍会无条件调它)。board_late_initialize 仍早于 INIT_ENTRYPOINT
  (nsh_main),故 /dev/console 及时,nsh 不受影响。
- 保留了 uart_rpmsg.c 的诊断 syslog(device_created armed / reannounce sent),待这次验证
  通过后再决定是否移除。
- 固件标记升 v3-phase;编译+打包通过(amp.img 19:00,hash fa1af583)。**待刷机验证**。


### UART 共用：第四次真机复盘 + 真正根因（isconsole EEXIST）
- **诊断版(v4-diag)日志**:`uart_rpmsg_init FAIL` + `syslog-test ret=-17`(=-EEXIST) +
  `/dev/ttyproxy exists`。且 syslog-test 出现→**syslog 可见**(排除"诊断不可见"),坐实
  device_created 是真没触发。
- **真正根因**:`uart_rpmsg_init(isconsole=true)` 先注册 `/dev/ttyproxy`(成功),再注册
  `/dev/console`——但 `/dev/console` **已被 arch serial 层占用**(CONFIG_DEV_CONSOLE=y,
  即便 SUPPRESS_UART_CONFIG),`uart_register` 返回 -EEXIST → init 走 `fail_with_rpmsg`
  分支,**把刚注册的 rpmsg callback 又 `rpmsg_unregister_callback` 注销了** → device_created
  永不触发 → endpoint 从未 announce → 无通道。前面几轮"相位""re-announce"都没打中,因为
  callback 根本被拆了。
- **修复**:`uart_rpmsg_init("linux","proxy",4096,false)`——isconsole=false,只注册
  /dev/ttyproxy 不碰 /dev/console,init 成功、callback 保留、device_created 触发、announce
  发出。固件标记 v5-noconsole,编译打包过(amp.img 19:17,hash 8daf97a5)。
- **遗留(下一步)**:isconsole=false 后 nsh 不在 /dev/ttyproxy 上。本版先验证"通道能否建立"
  (Linux 出现 creating channel rpmsg-ttyproxy + /dev/ttyNSH0)。通道确认通后,下一版再把
  nsh 绑到 /dev/ttyproxy(spawn 一个 nsh session,stdio 重定向到 /dev/ttyproxy)。


### UART 共用：通道打通（v5 验证）+ nsh 绑定（v6）
- **v5-noconsole 真机验证成功(通道打通)**:日志出现完整成功链条——
  `uart_rpmsg_init ok` + `syslog-test ret=0` + `[uart_rpmsg] device_created: ept armed` +
  Linux `[6.49s] creating channel rpmsg-ttyproxy` + `[15.3s] rpmsg_nsh_tty ...: new NuttX
  nsh channel 0x400 -> 0x400: ttyNSH0` + `/dev/ttyNSH0` 存在。**isconsole=false 修复确认有效,
  卡了 4 轮的通道终于建立。** (re-announce 一直报 ept not ready 走 else 分支,说明 device_created
  里 create_ept 的首次 announce 就送达了,re-announce 是冗余保险,无害。)
- **v6-altcondev 补 nsh 绑定**:isconsole=false 后 nsh 不在 /dev/ttyproxy 上。用 NSH 的
  ALTCONDEV 配置让 nsh 直接以 /dev/ttyproxy 为 console:
  defconfig 加 `CONFIG_NSH_ALTCONDEV=y` + `CONFIG_NSH_ALTSTDIN/OUT/ERR="/dev/ttyproxy"`。
  改 defconfig 后必须 make distclean + configure.sh 重生成 .config(已做,确认 ALTCONDEV=y 生效)。
  编译打包过(amp.img 19:22,hash c67eed24,nsh_altconsole.c 已编入)。**待真机验证 microcom 交互。**
- **ALTCONDEV 注意(影响 microcom 体验)**:非 /dev/console 时 (1) `\n` 不自动扩展 `\r\n`,
  (2) 输入不自动回显。microcom 端需开 local echo,换行可能要适配。


### UART 共用：交互成功 + 重连死锁修复（D 状态）
- **真机成功(v6)**:picocom 首次连接完整交互——`help` 列命令、`ls /` 列 /dev /proc、
  正常退出("Thanks for using picocom")。**UART 共用核心功能全线打通并验证。**
  标准连接命令:`picocom --imap lfcrlf --omap crlf /dev/ttyNSH0`(--omap crlf 让 Enter
  发的 \r 变 \r\n,nsh 认;不加则需 Ctrl-J 代替回车)。退出 Ctrl-A Ctrl-Q。
- **重连 bug(D 状态,已修)**:退出后二次连接 nsh 不响应、picocom 卡死进 `D`(不可中断
  睡眠)、`kill -9` 无效、只能重启。根因是**双向阻塞死锁**:
  - Linux `rpmsg_nsh_tty` RX 回调回 ack 用阻塞 `rpmsg_send`,TX 环满时不可中断死等;
  - NuttX `uart_rpmsg` `dmasend` 用 `rpmsg_get_tx_payload_buffer(wait=true)` 死等发送缓冲;
  - 二次连接数据累积时,一端等对端消费、对端等这端归还缓冲 → 互锁 → tty 卡死。
- **修复(两端都改非阻塞)**:
  - Linux `kernel/drivers/tty/rpmsg_nsh_tty.c`:ack 改 `rpmsg_trysend`(.ko md5
    0a7930d6, 20:03)。
  - NuttX `work/nuttx/drivers/serial/uart_rpmsg.c`:`dmasend` 的 get_tx_payload_buffer
    改 `wait=false`(拿不到缓冲丢本批,留在 dev->xmit 下次 dmatxavail 重试,不阻塞);
    `rxflowcontrol` 的 WAKEUP 改 `rpmsg_trysend`。
  - 固件 v7-nonblock,amp.img 20:14 hash 25ddebdf。**需两端同刷验证反复重连。**


### UART 共用：重连崩溃真相 = 内核内存损坏（非死锁）
- **抓栈定位**：`echo w`/`echo t` + `/proc/pid/stack` 显示 picocom 卡在 `tty_port_open`
  的 `port->mutex`，但持有者不在 blocked 列表。继续看到真正的事件：
  ```
  Unable to handle kernel paging request at 24ffffff8108bf22
  Internal error: Oops SMP;  pc: __kmem_cache_alloc_node
  Call trace: __kmalloc → __alloc_skb → unix_dgram_sendmsg (systemd-journal)
  Modules linked in: rpmsg_nsh_tty bcmdhd
  ```
  崩溃地址是**被覆写的 slab freelist 指针**（`ffffff81..` 高字节被 `0x24` 覆盖）——
  **堆越界写损坏了 slab**，systemd-journal 只是随机受害者。之前的 D 状态/卡死都是这个
  内存损坏的连锁后果，不是独立锁问题。
- **两个嫌疑 + 修复**：
  1. **echo server 遗留**（`rpmsg-ap3-ch0`）每秒 re-announce → Linux 反复
     `rpmsg_create_channel failed` 狂刷（日志 `8292 console messages dropped`）。反复触发
     失败/清理路径是强嫌疑。→ **从 evb7_amp_bringup.c 彻底删除 echo server**（使命早已完成）。
  2. **Linux `rpmsg_nsh_tty` 回 ack 时就地改 RX vring buffer 再 rpmsg_trysend**——把框架仍
     拥有的 RX buffer 当 TX payload 重发，可能破坏 rpmsg buffer 记账。→ 改为**独立栈 ack
     buffer**，不再触碰 RX buffer；加 cport NULL 检查、显式 int 边界运算。
- **清理**：去掉 uart_rpmsg.c / bringup.c 的临时诊断 syslog；bringup.c 精简重写（只剩
  amp_puts 状态行 + uart_rpmsg_init + rptun_init）。
- **产物**：NuttX v8-noecho，amp.img hash ff0edd25（20:37）;.ko md5 04cf4489（20:37）。
  **两侧必须同刷**（删 echo 在 NuttX 固件，独立 ack 在 .ko）。反复重连待验证。


### UART 共用：删 echo 后暴露 re-announce 鸡生蛋 bug（v9）
- **v8 真机**：无 Oops(内存损坏修复见效)、无刷屏(echo 已删),但 `/dev/ttyNSH0` 不出现——
  Linux 侧无 `creating channel rpmsg-ttyproxy`。
- **根因**：之前 echo 每秒 re-announce 会 kick mailbox doorbell,顺带让 Linux 处理 vring 里
  uart_rpmsg 的 announce;删 echo 后这个"顺带"没了。而 uart_rpmsg 自己的 re-announce work
  有个 `if (is_rpmsg_ept_ready(&ept))` 才发 NS 的门槛——但 `dest_addr` 要等 Linux 绑定才非
  ANY、Linux 绑定又要先收到 announce(doorbell) → **鸡生蛋,channel 永远建不起来**。之前一直
  靠 echo 的 doorbell 打破循环(所以 v5-v7 能成),echo 一删就露馅。
- **修复**：`uart_rpmsg.c` 的 re-announce work **无条件 `rpmsg_send_ns_message`**(去掉
  is_rpmsg_ept_ready 门槛),自己 kick doorbell,不再依赖 echo。
- **产物**：v9-doorbell,amp.img hash 52b042c9(20:42)。**只需重刷 NuttX 固件**;Linux .ko
  (md5 04cf4489)与 v8 相同,无需重换。


### UART 共用：重连崩溃真正根因 = tty_port 引用计数不平衡（UAF）——已修复并验证 [DONE]
- **前情**：v9-doorbell 通道终于稳定建立，首次连接完整交互 OK；但**重连必崩/卡死**。
  之前多轮把它当"死锁"和"堆内存被写坏（slab freelist 损坏）"来治（改非阻塞 trysend、
  删 echo、独立 ack buffer、path B 把 ack 从 RX 回调延迟到 work queue），都**没根治**——
  因为这些都是**表象**，不是根因。
- **抓到决定性证据**（板子 `/proc/<pid>/stack` + 崩溃 Oops）：
  ```
  picocom D 状态卡在: tty_port_open+0x94  (= mutex_lock(&port->mutex))
  随后 Oops:
    Unable to handle kernel paging request at ffffffc009d6f610
    pc : queued_spin_lock_slowpath+0x274/0x420
    lr : tty_port_open+0x16c/0x230
    Call trace: tty_port_open → rpmsg_nsh_tty_open → tty_open
  ```
  `tty_port_open` 在取 `port->lock` 自旋锁时锁结构里是垃圾指针 → `cport`(含 `port`)
  **已被 kfree**，重连还在用这块悬空内存 = **use-after-free**。之前的 slab 损坏 Oops
  和这次的 spinlock 崩溃，是**同一个 UAF** 的不同表现（释放后的 cport 被复用/写，
  污染 slab freelist，随机受害者是 systemd-journal）。
- **根因**：`rpmsg_nsh_tty_install()` 里只 `tty_port_install()`，**漏了 `tty_port_get()`**
  （kref 不 +1），而 `rpmsg_nsh_tty_cleanup()` 里有 `tty_port_put()`（kref -1）。于是每次
  open→close 循环 kref 净减 1：probe 后 kref=1，**第一次 picocom 退出时 cleanup 把 kref
  打到 0 → `destruct_port` → `kfree(cport)` 并从 idr 摘除**。第二次 picocom open 时
  `tty_port_open` 解引用已释放的 `port->lock` → 崩。"首次好、重连必崩"完全吻合。
- **对照上游确认**（`kernel/drivers/tty/rpmsg_tty.c`）：上游 `rpmsg_tty_install` 有
  `port = tty_port_get(&cport->port); return tty_port_install(port, ...)`——正是我漏的那句。
- **修复**（`kernel/drivers/tty/rpmsg_nsh_tty.c`，根因修复，非再打补丁）：
  `rpmsg_nsh_tty_install()` 加 `port = tty_port_get(&cport->port)` 与 cleanup 的
  `tty_port_put()` 配平；顺带加 `if (!cport) return -ENODEV` 防 idr 未命中解引用空指针。
- **path B 一并保留**（同一 .ko）：`handle_write` 不再在 RX 回调里同步 `rpmsg_trysend`，
  改为记录 result/cookie 后 `schedule_work`，由 `rpmsg_nsh_ack_work()` 在进程上下文发 ack
  （单槽即可：NuttX 一发一等、ept_cb 不校验 cookie）。probe 里 `INIT_WORK`、remove 里
  `cancel_work_sync`。这消除了"RX 上下文重入 rpmsg TX"这一 slab 损坏来源；虽非重连崩溃
  的根因（根因是 kref UAF），但属正确实践，保留。
- **产物**：.ko md5 **4f22002e**（root-cause 版；path B 中间版 09f40736 作废，v8 的
  04cf4489 更早作废）。**只需换 .ko，NuttX 固件仍 v9-doorbell（amp.img 52b042c9）不动。**
- **真机验证 PASSED**：重启清掉被 UAF 污染的内核状态 → insmod 新 .ko →
  `picocom --imap lfcrlf --omap crlf /dev/ttyNSH0` **反复连断多轮均正常进 nsh、正常退出，
  不再卡 D 状态，dmesg 无 `Unable to handle kernel paging request` / 无 tty_port_open Oops**。
  用户确认"正常了"。**UART 共用（一根 UART2 线，Linux ↔ NuttX nsh 切换）功能 + 重连稳定性
  全线打通。**
- **方法论教训**：`tty_port_open+偏移` 的偏移能精确区分卡点（0x94=mutex_lock，尾部大偏移
  才是 block_til_ready 的可中断等待）；D 状态(不可中断)≠ 普通阻塞，指向锁/UAF 而非流控；
  "首次好、第 N 次崩"是**释放后重用/引用计数不平衡**的典型签名，应优先查 get/put 配平，
  而不是反复在数据路径上打补丁。


## 探索：把 PMU M0 作为第三个 AMP 域点亮（第一步，代码就绪，待硬件验证）
目标：在现有 Linux(7×A55) + NuttX(cpu_l3) 之上，把空闲的 PMU Cortex-M0 也拉起来跑
一份独立固件（AMP 三域）。第一步只做“最小 heartbeat 点亮”，验证 u-boot 拉 M0 的链路
现成可用 + M0 真的在执行。A55 NuttX 完全不动（并存，非切换）。

### 关键调查结论（u-boot 侧拉 M0 链路已现成，无需查 TRM）
- u-boot `drivers/cpu/rockchip_amp.c`：`amp_cpus_on → brought_up_amp`。按 FIT image 的
  **type** 分流：`arch=arm64` 走 `psci_cpu_on`（A55，现在拉 NuttX 用的）；
  **`type=IH_TYPE_STANDALONE` 走 `standalone_handler → fit_standalone_ext_release`**（M0）。
- `arch/arm/mach-rockchip/rk3588/rk3588.c:1435 fit_standalone_ext_release()` 注释即
  “pmu m0 configuration”：设 GPLL(`0xfd7c0000+0x1c4`)、经 **SIP `0x82000028`**
  (`sip_smc_mcu_config`, mcu_id=`PMUMCU_0=0x10`, func=`MCU_CODE_START_ADDR=0x01`,
  值=`0xffff0000|(load>>16)`，64KB 对齐)设 M0 取指起始地址、写 `0xfd7f0a00`
  (`PMU1CRU_SOFTRST_CON00`) `0x20000000` **清 bit13 解复位 M0**。
  （bit13 = SRST_F_PMU_CM0_CORE，与本文件早前 M0 复位调查完全对上。）
- 依赖 vendor BL31 v1.54 实现该 SIP（闭源，无法源码确认；但与拉 A55 的
  `sip_smc_amp_cfg` 同套 vendor SIP，大概率支持；100% 确认要上板看串口）。
- 工具链齐：系统 `/usr/bin/arm-none-eabi-gcc` + openvela prebuilts 均有 arm-none-eabi。
- M0 保留内存现成：`rk3588-amp.dtsi` 的 `mcu_reserved: mcu@7a00000`（物理
  **0x07a00000, 2MB, no-map**），evb7 `-amp.dts` 已 include。**第一步不改任何 Linux dts**，
  纯 amp.img，可逆性最好。

### 本次改动（external，未提交）
- 新增 `rk3588-amp-demo/m0/`：
  - `m0_start.S`：ARMv6-M/Thumb 最小固件。vector(SP=0x07bf0000, reset+1) + reset：
    写 magic `0x414D5030`→`0x07a10000`，counter++ →`0x07a10004`，delay 循环。
  - `m0.ld`：链接基址 `0x07a00000`（假设 CODE_START 即取指基址/vector 在物理 load 处）。
  - `build-m0.sh`：`arm-none-eabi-gcc -mcpu=cortex-m0 -mthumb -nostartfiles -nostdlib`。
  - 产物 `m0.bin` = **40 字节**（反汇编核对正确）。
- 新增 `rk3588-amp-demo/amp-m0.its`：A55 NuttX(`amp1`, firmware/arm64/cpu=0x300/
  load=0x30000000) + M0(`mcu`, **type=standalone**/arch=arm/thumb=1/load=0x07a00000)
  并存，`loadables = "amp1","mcu"`。
- `amp.img` 重打包（`mkimage -f amp-m0.its -E -p 0xe00`）：dumpimage 确认含 amp1(hash
  52b042c9 未变) + mcu(40B, load 0x07a00000)。A55-only 旧 amp.img 已备份至
  `rk3588-amp-demo/_a55_backup/amp.img.a55-nuttx-v9`（md5 361e200b）。

### 关键假设（上板要验证的点）
- **M0 取指语义**：假设 CODE_START(0x07a00000) 即 M0 vector/取指基址（固件链接在此）。
  若上板 M0 不动，改假设为“M0 remap 地址0→CODE_START”，把 `m0.ld` ORIGIN 改 `0x0`
  重链重打包（数据地址仍用物理 0x07a1xxxx，数据访问不 remap）。

### 待硬件验证（烧 amp 分区，A55/kernel/uboot 都不动）
```
rkdeveloptool db /media/1t/openvela/u-boot/rk3588_spl_loader_v1.21.114.bin
rkdeveloptool wlx amp /media/1t/openvela/rk3588-amp-demo/amp.img
rkdeveloptool rd
```
- 一级（链路，不依赖 remap 猜对）：u-boot 串口出现 `AMP: Handle standalone: 'pmu-m0'
  at 0x07a00000 ...OK`，且 A55 `AMP: Brought up cpu[300] ...OK` 仍在。
- 二级（M0 执行，依赖 remap 假设）：Linux 起来 `busybox devmem 0x07a10000 32`=0x414D5030、
  `devmem 0x07a10004 32` 读两次递增 → M0 活了 + 链接基址假设正确。
- 同时确认 A55 无恙：`picocom --imap lfcrlf --omap crlf /dev/ttyNSH0` 进 nsh、nproc=7。
- 回退 A55-only：`rkdeveloptool wlx amp _a55_backup/amp.img.a55-nuttx-v9`。


### M0 点亮：第一次硬件验证结果 —— 拉核链路 PASS，M0 未执行（remap 假设错）→ 已出 v2
**一级 PASS（链路现成，坐实）**：u-boot 串口
```
AMP: Brought up cpu[300] with state 0x12, entry 0x30000000 ...OK   ← A55 NuttX 照常
Handle standalone: 'pmu-m0' at 0x07a00000 ...OK                     ← M0 拉核链路 OK
Sysmem Warn: "mcu@7a00000"(0x07a00000-0x07c00000) overlap "pmu-m0"(0x07a00000-0x07a00028)
allocated.rgn[3].name = "pmu-m0"
```
→ **vendor BL31 v1.54 确实支持 SIP 0x82000028**（CODE_START + 解复位全 OK）。0x28=40B 正是
m0.bin 大小。两条 Sysmem Warn 是“镜像落在自己保留区内”，与 A55 的 nuttx-cpu3 overlap
amp-core 同性质，正常。

**A55 零回归**：`[AMP] hello from NuttX on cpu_l3` + `build=nsh-over-rpmsg v9-doorbell` +
`rptun init ok`；Linux `smp: Brought up 1 node, 7 CPUs`（cpu_l3 仍归 AMP）；
`creating channel rpmsg-ttyproxy` 照常 → **并存策略成立**。

**二级 FAIL（M0 没跑）**：`busybox devmem 0x07a10000 32` → `0xFFFFFFFF`（期望 0x414D5030），
`0x07a10004` 连读 4 次全 `0xFFFFFFFF` 不变 → 该内存**从未被写过**（no-map 区未初始化态，
与早前读 mcu@7a00000 得 0xFBFFFFFF 同类）。

**根因 = 地址模型假设错（v1 已预留的风险点）**：
- v1 把代码/向量链在 CODE_START=0x07a00000，reset 向量填**物理** 0x07a00009。
- 实际 **M0 从自己的地址 0 取向量**（ARMv6-M 规范），硬件把该低 64KB 窗口 remap 到 CODE_START。
- 证据：u-boot 只把 **高16位** 传给 SIP（`0xffff0000 | (load>>16)`）= 典型 **64KB remap 窗口基址**
  语义，而非绝对 PC。
- 于是 M0 读到 0x07a00009 → 跳到自己视角未映射的地址 → 取指异常，一个字节都没写出来。

**v2 修正（已编译打包，待烧录）**：
- `m0.ld`：ORIGIN `0x07a00000` → **`0x00000000`**（代码/向量用 M0 视角）。
- `m0_start.S`：初始 SP `0x07bf0000` → **`0x0000f000`**（窗口内）；**数据地址仍用物理
  `0x07a10000/0x07a10004`**（remap 只覆盖取指窗口；M0 作为总线 master 的 load/store 走真实
  物理地址，这也是 Linux 能用 /dev/mem 看到同一块的前提）。
- 核对：`m0.bin` 头 8 字节 = `0000f000 00000009`（v1 是 `07bf0000 07a00009`）；反汇编仍见
  `.word 0x07a10000`。m0.bin 仍 40B。
- `amp.img` 重打包 md5 **beb74279**；mcu hash `1fba9802`（变），**nuttx-cpu3 hash 仍
  `52b042c9` = A55 一字未动**。
- 烧录/验证/回退步骤同上节。


### M0 点亮：v2 也是 0xFFFFFFFF —— 真根因 = its 缺 uc_start/uc_end，走进 __weak 空桩（v3 已修）
**v2 硬件结果**：`devmem 0x07a10000/0x07a10004` 仍全 `0xFFFFFFFF`；拉核行仍 PASS
（`Handle standalone: 'pmu-m0' at 0x07a00000 ...OK`），A55 NuttX 照常。

**真根因（读 u-boot 源码确认，非猜测）**：`drivers/cpu/rockchip_amp.c standalone_handler()`
按属性分流：
```c
if (sram_start || exsram_start || experi_start || uc_start || uc_end)
        ret = fit_standalone_ext_release(id, args);  /* 真正配 M0 + 解复位 */
else
        ret = fit_standalone_release(id, entry_point); /* RK3588 未实现 */
```
- RK3588 **只实现 `fit_standalone_ext_release`**（rk3588.c:1435）；`fit_standalone_release`
  在 rockchip_amp.c:220 是 `__weak { return 0; }` **空桩**。
- v1/v2 的 its **五个属性一个都没设** → 走 else → 空桩 → **CODE_START 从未编程、M0 复位从未解除**。
- 日志那句 `...OK` 是空桩 `return 0` 打出来的 —— **纯假阳性**。
- ⇒ **v1/v2 关于“地址模型/remap 假设”的结论全部作废**：M0 连执行机会都没有，两次测的都不是
  地址模型。（教训：先确认“代码路径是否真的被执行”，再推断“执行后行为为何不对”；
  一个打印 OK 不等于该功能真的跑了 —— 与本文件早前 `tty_port_open+0x94`、
  `IGROUPR` RAZ 那两次误判同类。）

**v3 修正（已编译打包，待烧录）**：
- `amp-m0.its` mcu 节点新增 **`uc_start = <0x07a00000>` / `uc_end = <0x07c00000>`**
  （mcu_reserved 全域）→ 路由到 `fit_standalone_ext_release`，它才会
  设 GPLL、经 SIP 0x82000028 设 CODE_START、写 `0xfd7f0a00` 清 bit13 解复位；
  顺带把该区标为 M0 非缓存，写入直达 DDR，Linux /dev/mem 可见。
- `m0_start.S`：heartbeat 目标从远处物理 `0x07a10000` 改为**代码窗口内** M0 偏移
  **`0x800`（→ 物理 0x07a00800）**，复刻已验证的 A55 裸机 demo（load+0x800 = 0x30000800）
  模式；避免赌“M0 能否访问远处物理地址”，一次坏 store 会 HardFault 锁死核、counter 再也不动。
- 代码/向量仍链在 `0x00000000`（ARMv6-M **无 VTOR**，向量表固定在地址 0，硬件必须把
  CODE_START 映到那里）；向量表头 8 字节 `0000f000 00000009`。m0.bin 仍 40B。
- `amp.img` md5 **d0d00176**；`fdtget` 复核镜像内 `uc_start=0x07A00000, uc_end=0x07C00000,
  load=0x07A00000`；nuttx-cpu3 hash 仍 `52b042c9`（A55 一字未动）。

**新验证地址**（注意变了）：
```
busybox devmem 0x07a00800 32   → 期望 0x414D5030
busybox devmem 0x07a00804 32   → 期望递增
```
烧录/回退同前。若这版仍不动，下一步不再改固件，而是先在 Linux 侧只读确认
`devmem 0xfd7f0a00`（PMU1CRU_SOFTRST_CON00）bit13 是否已被清 0，直接判定“复位到底解没解”，
把问题二分到 u-boot 侧还是 M0 侧。


### 里程碑：PMU Cortex-M0 点亮成功（v3 真机验证 PASSED）—— 第三个 AMP 域跑起来了
**真机结果（全部达成）**：
```
busybox devmem 0x07a00800 32  ->  0x414D5030            magic "AMP0"，精确匹配
busybox devmem 0x07a00804 32  ->  0x0B -> 0x0C -> 0x0D  counter 持续递增
busybox devmem 0xfd7f0a00 32  ->  0x00000000            bit13=0，M0 复位已解除
```
counter 约 1~2s 一格，与 `0x300000` 延时循环 @200MHz 量级吻合 ⇒ **PMU Cortex-M0 正在执行
我们的固件**。（`0xfd7f0a00`=PMU1CRU_SOFTRST_CON00 读 0，直接坐实 u-boot 解复位生效。）

**A55 域零回归（并存成立）**：`AMP: Brought up cpu[300] ... entry 0x30000000 ...OK`；
`[AMP] hello from NuttX on cpu_l3` / `build=nsh-over-rpmsg v9-doorbell` / `rptun init ok`；
`rpmsg_nsh_tty ...: new NuttX nsh channel 0x400 -> 0x400: ttyNSH0`（UART 共用照常）。

**本板现在同时跑三个独立域**：
| 域 | 核 | 系统 |
|----|----|------|
| 1 | A55 ×7 | Linux（桌面/GPU/WiFi/BT） |
| 2 | A55 ×1 (cpu_l3) | NuttX + nsh over rpmsg（UART 共用） |
| 3 | **Cortex-M0 (PMU)** | **裸机 heartbeat（本次新增）** |

**生效配置速查（M0 点亮最小充分集）**：
- its mcu 节点：`type="standalone"`, `arch="arm"`, `load=<0x07a00000>`,
  **`uc_start=<0x07a00000>`, `uc_end=<0x07c00000>`**（缺 uc_* 会静默走 __weak 空桩！）
- 固件：ARMv6-M/Thumb，代码+向量链在 **0x00000000**（无 VTOR，向量表固定在地址 0，
  硬件把 CODE_START 映射到此），向量表 = `0000f000`(SP) + `00000009`(reset|thumb)；
  heartbeat 写 **M0 偏移 0x800 → 物理 0x07a00800**（窗口内，安全）。
- 产物：m0.bin 40B；amp.img md5 **d0d00176**（nuttx-cpu3 hash 仍 52b042c9）。
- 回退 A55-only：`rkdeveloptool wlx amp _a55_backup/amp.img.a55-nuttx-v9`。

**推翻早前的悲观判断（重要留档）**：最初结论是“M0 需要 TRM 才能拿 boot-addr 寄存器、
rkbin 无 RK3588 M0 loader、boot 链不加载 M0，基本走不通”。**实际 vendor u-boot + BL31 已把
整条拉核链封装完备**（standalone → fit_standalone_ext_release → SIP 0x82000028 设 CODE_START
+ 写 CRU 解复位），完全不需要 TRM。唯一真实门槛是一个不显眼的分派条件：**its 不设
uc_start/uc_end 等属性就会静默落入 `__weak` 空桩，且照样打印 "...OK"**。
方法论教训（与本文件早前 `tty_port_open+0x94` 偏移误判、`IGROUPR` RAZ 读 0 误判同类）：
**先确认目标代码路径是否真被执行，再解释“执行结果为何不对”；一句 OK 不等于功能真的跑了。**

**下一步（可选，第二步）**：M0 上加 rpmsg（抄 rk3576-amp.dtsi 的 `MCU: link-id 0x04` +
mailbox 通道），实现 Linux ↔ M0 通信；M0 中断走 INTMUX→NVIC，天然绕开 GIC 安全域问题。


## TRM 到手：PMU_M0 三大未知量全部查清（推翻此前若干猜测）
拿到 `Rockchip RK3588 TRM V1.0-Part1/Part2-20220309.pdf`（Part1 147k 行 / Part2 239k 行文本）。
之前只有 datasheet（无任何寄存器地址映射），关键问题只能靠读 vendor u-boot 源码 + 上板实测反推。
TRM 一次性给出答案：

### Table 9-3 PMU_M0 Address Remap（决定性）
M0 见到 **6 个固定 512MB 窗口**，各由一个 `pmu1_sgrf_soc_conN[15:0]` 重定位（**粒度 64KB**，
正是 u-boot 传 `load>>16` 的原因）。`PMU1_SGRF` 基址 = **0xFD582000**（8KB）。
| M0 视角 | 大小 | 类型 | 用途 | 重定位 |
|---------|------|------|------|--------|
| 0x00000000+ | 512MB | Normal **WT** | ROM/flash（**取指**） | `soc_con9` ← u-boot CODE_START |
| 0x20000000+ | 512MB | Normal WBWA | 片内 RAM | `soc_con10` ← SRAM_START |
| 0x40000000+ | 512MB | Device XN | 外设 | **固定 → 物理 0xF0000000** |
| 0x60000000+ | 512MB | Normal WBWA(写回) | 片外 DDR | `soc_con11` |
| 0x80000000+ | 512MB | Normal **WT**(写通) | 片外 DDR | `soc_con11` |
| 0xA0000000+ | 1GB | Device XN | 外设 | `soc_con12` ← EXPERI_START |
原文注：**remap 要等 MCU 软复位后才生效** → 正对应 u-boot「先 SIP 设地址、最后写 CRU 解复位」，
我们的流程本来就是对的。SGRF 是 secure 寄存器 → 这也解释了为何必须走 BL31 的 SMC 而非 NS 直写。

### 三个未知量的答案（都推翻了之前的保守/错误判断）
1. **取指窗口 = 512MB，不是 64KB** ⇒ **NuttX 镜像大小完全不是约束**。此前 `m0.ld` 里
